        CompareDifference(TestCount - initialTestCount, 1551)
        functionCount += 1

    End Sub '"

    Private Sub Nastran2(testFilesFolder As String, ByRef functionCount As Integer)
        Dim initialTestCount As Integer = TestCount

        Dim model As ModelData

        'Fixed support
        'Force
        'All allowed (symmetric) section shapes
        'Line2 beam
        If SolveFile("NAS B30 beam all shapes.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            If model.Modes.Count = 0 Then
                For i As Integer = 1 To 15
                    CompareTrue(False)
                Next
            Else
                'Pcr = pi^2 EI/(KL)^2
                Dim pIy As Double = Math.PI ^ 2 * 200000000000.0 * 0.0000533641666666667 / (2 * 25) ^ 2
                Dim pIz As Double = Math.PI ^ 2 * 200000000000.0 * 0.00000564416666666666 / (2 * 25) ^ 2
                Dim pRecty As Double = Math.PI ^ 2 * 200000000000.0 * 0.00005625 / (2 * 25) ^ 2
                Dim pRectz As Double = Math.PI ^ 2 * 200000000000.0 * 0.0001 / (2 * 25) ^ 2
                Dim pRectTubey As Double = Math.PI ^ 2 * 200000000000.0 * 0.00012072 / (2 * 25) ^ 2
                Dim pRectTubez As Double = Math.PI ^ 2 * 200000000000.0 * 0.00006392 / (2 * 25) ^ 2
                Dim pCirc As Double = Math.PI ^ 2 * 200000000000.0 * 0.0000785398163397448 / (2 * 25) ^ 2
                Dim pCircTube As Double = Math.PI ^ 2 * 200000000000.0 * 0.0000270098428392383 / (2 * 25) ^ 2
                Dim pGeneraly As Double = Math.PI ^ 2 * 200000000000.0 * 0.0003 / (2 * 25) ^ 2
                Dim pGeneralz As Double = Math.PI ^ 2 * 200000000000.0 * 0.000006 / (2 * 25) ^ 2

                CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), pIz / 5, 1)
                CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(2), pGeneralz / 5, 1)
                CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(3), pCircTube / 5, 1)
                CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(4), pCircTube / 5, 1)
                CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(5), pIy / 5, 1)
                CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(6), pRecty / 5, 1)
                CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(7), pRectTubez / 5, 1)
                'Modes 8 and 9 are higher order modes
                CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(10), pCirc / 5, 1)
                CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(11), pCirc / 5, 1)
                CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(12), pRectz / 5, 1)

                'Compare angle error of direction of displacement vector.
                CompareDifference(AngleFrom({10}, 1, AxisType.Z), 0)
                CompareDifference(AngleFrom({11}, 2, AxisType.Z), 0)
                CompareDifference(AngleFrom({10}, 5, AxisType.Y), 0)
                CompareDifference(AngleFrom({2}, 6, AxisType.Y), 0)
                CompareDifference(AngleFrom({2}, 12, AxisType.Z), 0)
            End If

        End If

        If SolveFile("NAS B30 beam thermal stress.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            If model.Modes.Count = 0 Then
                For i As Integer = 1 To 4
                    CompareTrue(False)
                Next
            Else
                Dim criticalForce As Double = 0.25 * Math.PI ^ 2 * 200000000000.0 * 0.0000000000000833333333333333 / 0.1 ^ 2
                Dim fRef As Double = criticalForce / (0.00012 * (400 - 300) * 200000000000.0 * 0.000001)
                CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), fRef, 0.1)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 2, 1), 0)
                CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(2), fRef, 0.1)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 2, 2), 0)
            End If
        End If


        For Each quadElementType As Integer In {1, 2, 4}

            Select Case quadElementType
                Case 1
                    Messages.Append(vbNewLine & "MIN4" & vbNewLine)
                    NastranOptions.Min4T = False
                    NastranOptions.Mitc4Plus = False
                Case 2
                    Messages.Append(vbNewLine & "MIN4T" & vbNewLine)
                    NastranOptions.Min4T = True
                    NastranOptions.Mitc4Plus = False
                Case 4
                    Messages.Append(vbNewLine & "MITC4+" & vbNewLine)
                    NastranOptions.Min4T = False
                    NastranOptions.Mitc4Plus = True
            End Select

            'Quad4
            'Distorted
            'Membrane and bending modes
            'Rotated in space
            If SolveFile("NAS B30 quad4 column.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
                'Reference value from refined mesh in CCX and internal solver.
                Dim fRef As Double = 27.05

                'Mode 1 is the 1st bending mode
                Select Case quadElementType
                    Case 1, 2
                        CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), fRef, 4)
                    Case 4 '2% vs 4% is important to distinguish the bugfix of not initializing SSS for KED in MITC4+ in Mystran.
                        CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), fRef, 2)
                End Select

                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 4, 1) / NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4, 1), 1)

                'Mode 3 is the 1st in-plane mode with huge error in buckling factor which is OK because of coarse mesh.
                'Compare to the existing incorrect value to detect changes.
                Select Case quadElementType
                    Case 1, 2
                        CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(3), 283.9479, 0)
                    Case 4 'Reference value same as internal solver to 3 s.f.
                        CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(3), 423.7345, 0)
                End Select

                CompareRatioSingle(-NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 4, 3) / NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4, 3), 1)

            End If

            If SolveFile("NAS B30 quad4 LTB.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
                'Reference value from refined quad8 mesh in CCX and internal solver.
                Dim fRef As Double = 4.362

                'Mode 1 is lateral
                Dim tolerance As Double
                Select Case quadElementType
                    Case 1 : tolerance = 0.3 'MIN4
                    Case 2 : tolerance = 0.6 'MIN4T converges to 4.34 instead of 4.36
                    Case 4 : tolerance = 1 'MITC4+ It converges with refinement
                End Select
                CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), fRef, tolerance)
                CompareDifference(AngleFrom({24}, 1, AxisType.Z), 0)

            End If

            If SolveFile("NAS B30 quad4 nonuniform stress.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
                'Reference solution from refined quad8 mesh in CCX and internal solver.
                Dim fRef As Double = 63770.0

                'Mode 1 is lateral
                CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), fRef, 3)
                CompareDifference(AngleFrom({24}, 1, AxisType.Z), 0)

                Dim uZratio As Double = NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 3, 1) _
                                      / NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 2, 1)
                CompareRatio(uZratio, -0.000151269637949346 / -0.000126614160823913, 2)

            End If

            If SolveFile("NAS B30 quad4 thermal stress.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
                'I = bh^3/12
                Dim I As Double = 0.025 * 0.001 ^ 3 / 12
                'F_cr = n pi^2 E I / L^2, n=0.25
                Dim criticalForce As Double = 0.25 * Math.PI ^ 2 * 50000000000.0 * I / 0.2 ^ 2
                'buckling factor = F_cr / cte * deltaT * E * A
                Dim fRef As Double = criticalForce / (0.0006 * (400 - 300) * 50000000000.0 * 0.000025)
                Dim tolerance As Double
                Select Case quadElementType
                    Case 1, 2 : tolerance = 0.5
                    Case 4 : tolerance = 0.7
                End Select
                CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), fRef, tolerance)
                CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(2), fRef, tolerance)
                CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(3), fRef, tolerance)
            End If

            'Tests transformed coordinates with buckling bug in Mystran
            If SolveFile("NAS B30 quad4 thermal stress transformed.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
                'I = bh^3/12
                Dim I As Double = 0.025 * 0.001 ^ 3 / 12
                'F_cr = n pi^2 E I / L^2, n=0.25
                Dim criticalForce As Double = 0.25 * Math.PI ^ 2 * 50000000000.0 * I / 0.2 ^ 2
                'buckling factor = F_cr / cte * deltaT * E * A
                Dim fRef As Double = criticalForce / (0.0006 * (400 - 300) * 50000000000.0 * 0.000025)
                Dim tolerance As Double
                Select Case quadElementType
                    Case 1, 2 : tolerance = 0.5
                    Case 4 : tolerance = 0.7
                End Select
                CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), fRef, tolerance)
                CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(2), fRef, tolerance)
                CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(3), fRef, tolerance)
            End If

            'Tests that https://github.com/MYSTRANsolver/MYSTRAN/issues/129 is fixed
            If SolveFile("NAS B30 quad4 thin.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
                'I = bh^3/12
                Dim I As Double = 0.01 * 0.00001 ^ 3 / 12
                'F_cr = n pi^2 E I / L^2, n=0.25
                Dim criticalForce As Double = 0.25 * Math.PI ^ 2 * 50000000000.0 * I / 0.2 ^ 2
                'buckling factor = F_cr / F
                Dim fRef As Double = criticalForce / 1
                CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), fRef, 0.7)
                CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(2), fRef, 0.7)
            End If

            NastranOptions.Min4T = False
            NastranOptions.Mitc4Plus = False

        Next



        'Tri3
        'Distorted
        'Membrane and bending modes
        'Rotated in space
        If SolveFile("NAS B30 tri3 column.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Reference value from refined mesh in CCX and internal solver.
            Dim fRef As Double = 27.05

            'Mode 1 is the 1st bending mode
            CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), fRef, 4)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 4, 1), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4, 1))

            'Mode 2 is the 1st in-plane mode with huge error in buckling factor which is OK because of coarse mesh.
            'Compare to the existing incorrect value to detect changes.
            CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(2), 138.5563, 0)
            CompareRatioSingle(-NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 4, 2), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4, 2))
        End If

        If SolveFile("NAS B30 tri3 LTB.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Reference value from refined quad8 mesh in CCX and internal solver.
            Dim fRef As Double = 4.362

            'Mode 1 is lateral
            CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), fRef, 4) ' TRIA3 Converges with further refinement.
            CompareDifference(AngleFrom({24}, 1, AxisType.Z), 0)

        End If

        If SolveFile("NAS B30 tri3 nonuniform stress.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Reference solution from refined quad8 mesh in CCX and internal solver.
            Dim fRef As Double = 63770.0

            'Mode 1 is lateral
            CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), fRef, 3)
            CompareDifference(AngleFrom({24}, 1, AxisType.Z), 0)

            Dim uZratio As Double = NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 3, 1) _
                                  / NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 2, 1)
            CompareRatio(uZratio, -0.000151269637949346 / -0.000126614160823913, 2)

        End If

        If SolveFile("NAS B30 tri3 thermal stress.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'I = bh^3/12
            Dim I As Double = 0.025 * 0.001 ^ 3 / 12
            'F_cr = n pi^2 E I / L^2, n=0.25
            Dim criticalForce As Double = 0.25 * Math.PI ^ 2 * 50000000000.0 * I / 0.2 ^ 2
            'buckling factor = F_cr / cte * deltaT * E * A
            Dim fRef As Double = criticalForce / (0.0006 * (400 - 300) * 50000000000.0 * 0.000025)
            CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), fRef, 0.5)
            CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(2), fRef, 0.5)
            CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(3), fRef, 0.5)
        End If

        'Tests that https://github.com/MYSTRANsolver/MYSTRAN/issues/129 is fixed
        If SolveFile("NAS B30 tri3 thin.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'I = bh^3/12
            Dim I As Double = 0.01 * 0.00001 ^ 3 / 12
            'F_cr = n pi^2 E I / L^2, n=0.25
            Dim criticalForce As Double = 0.25 * Math.PI ^ 2 * 50000000000.0 * I / 0.2 ^ 2
            'buckling factor = F_cr / F
            Dim fRef As Double = criticalForce / 1
            If model.Modes.Count = 0 Then
                CompareTrue(False)
                CompareTrue(False)
            Else
                CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), fRef, 0.7)
                CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(2), fRef, 0.7)
            End If
        End If



        'These go wrong with in Mystran with IORQ2T at default (3). Require 2.

        If SolveFile("NAS S30 shell composite force bug.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            Dim refY As Double = NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 4)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 3), refY, 0)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 8), refY, 0)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 7), refY, 0)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 12), refY, 0)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 11), refY, 0)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 16), refY, 0)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 15), refY, 0)

            Dim refZ As Double = NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 3), refZ, 0)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 8), refZ, 0)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 7), refZ, 0)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 12), refZ, 0)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 11), refZ, 0)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 16), refZ, 0)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 15), refZ, 0)

            'Compare to internal solver refined quadratic mesh.
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4), -0.000112033195021115)
        End If

        If SolveFile("NAS S30 shell composite force bug Z force.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            Dim refY As Double = NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 4)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 3), refY, 0)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 8), refY, 0)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 7), refY, 0)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 12), refY, 0)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 11), refY, 0)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 16), refY, 0)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 15), refY, 0)

            Dim refZ As Double = NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 3), refZ, 0)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 8), refZ, 0)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 7), refZ, 0)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 12), refZ, 0)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 11), refZ, 0)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 16), refZ, 0)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 15), refZ, 0)
        End If



        'Tests Mystran bug with rotated nodes coordinates on BAR in buckling
        If SolveFile("NAS B30 beam rotated constraint.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Reference solution from model aligned with axis
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), 4.111297)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 2, 1), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 2, 1))
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 2, 1), 0, 0.000000000001)
            CompareDifference(OutlineTreeErrorCount({"Analysis <Buckling>"}), 1)
            CompareOutlineTreeError("Mystran is a feature rich linear solver, but as of 2025 it lacks exhaustive validation and still has some known bugs. A significant validation effort is planned for 2026.", {"Analysis <Buckling>"})
        End If


        Messages.Append("Number of tests")
        CompareDifference(TestCount - initialTestCount, 146)
        functionCount += 1

    End Sub '"

    Private Sub Nastran3(testFilesFolder As String, ByRef functionCount As Integer)
        Dim initialTestCount As Integer = TestCount

        Dim model As ModelData

        For Each quadElementType As Integer In {1, 2}

            Select Case quadElementType
                Case 1
                    Messages.Append(vbNewLine & "MIN4" & vbNewLine)
                    NastranOptions.Min4T = False
                Case 2
                    Messages.Append(vbNewLine & "MIN4T" & vbNewLine)
                    NastranOptions.Min4T = True
            End Select

            'All 5 force per length and 3 moment per length components on CQUAD4
            'Axis aligned
            If SolveFile("NAS S30 shell force per length components quad4.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
                'Force UU, VV uniform
                CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, 1, 1), 1 / 2)
                CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, 1, 3), 1 / 2)
                CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, 1, 2), 1 / 2)
                CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, 1, 2), 2 / 2)
                CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, 1, 4), 2 / 2)
                CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, 1, 3), 2 / 2)
                'Force UV uniform
                CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, 2, 1), 5 / 2)
                CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, 2, 2), 5 / 2)
                CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, 2, 3), 5 / 2)
                'Bending about U - no force
                CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, 3, 1), 0, 0.000000000000001)
                CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, 3, 2), 0, 0.000000000000001)
                CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, 3, 3), 0, 0.000000000000001)
                CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthVW, 3, 4), 0, 0.000000000000001)
                CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthWU, 3, 1), 0, 0.000000000000001)
                'Bending about V - no force
                CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, 4, 1), 0, 0.000000000000001)
                CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, 4, 2), 0, 0.000000000000001)
                CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, 4, 3), 0, 0.000000000000001)
                CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthVW, 4, 4), 0, 0.000000000000001)
                CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthWU, 4, 1), 0, 0.000000000000001)
                'Force VW uniform
                CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthVW, 5, 4), 0.003 / 2, If(quadElementType = 2, 1, SinglePercent))
                'Force WU uniform
                CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthWU, 6, 1), 0.003 / 2, If(quadElementType = 2, 3, SinglePercent))
                'Moment V
                CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthV, 3, 2), -0.002 / 2)
                'Moment U
                CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthU, 4, 3), 0.002 / 2)
                'Moment UV. Half the applied moment because there are two of them - UV and VU.
                CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthUV, 7, 4), -0.002 / 2 / 2)
            End If

            NastranOptions.Min4T = False

        Next


        If SolveFile("NAS S30 shell force per length components tri3.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Force UU, VV uniform
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, 1, 1), 1 / 2)
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, 7, 3), 1 / 2)
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, 1, 2), 1 / 2)
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, 7, 2), 2 / 2)
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, 7, 1), 2 / 2)
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, 1, 3), 2 / 2)
            CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, 1, 1), 0, 0.000000000000001)
            CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, 7, 2), 0, 0.000000000000001)
            'Force UV uniform
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, 2, 1), 5 / 2)
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, 2, 2), 5 / 2)
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, 8, 3), 5 / 2)
            'Bending about U - no force
            CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, 3, 1), 0, 0.000000000000001)
            CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, 3, 2), 0, 0.000000000000001)
            CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, 3, 3), 0, 0.000000000000001)
            CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthVW, 3, 1), 0, 0.000000000000001)
            CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthWU, 3, 2), 0, 0.000000000000001)
            'Bending about V - no force
            CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, 4, 1), 0, 0.000000000000001)
            CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, 4, 2), 0, 0.000000000000001)
            CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, 4, 3), 0, 0.000000000000001)
            CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthVW, 4, 2), 0, 0.000000000000001)
            CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthWU, 4, 1), 0, 0.000000000000001)
            'Force VW uniform. Average of both elements is better than each one individually.
            CompareRatioSingle((ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthVW, 5, 1) + ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthVW, 11, 1)) / 2, 0.003 / 2)
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthVW, 11, 2), 0.003 / 2, 2)
            'Force WU uniform. Average of both elements is better than each one individually.
            CompareRatioSingle((ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthWU, 6, 3) + ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthWU, 12, 3)) / 2, 0.003 / 2)
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthWU, 12, 1), 0.003 / 2, 2)
            'Moment V
            CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthU, 3, 1), 0, 0.000000000000001)
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthV, 3, 2), -0.002 / 2)
            CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthU, 9, 3), 0, 0.000000000000001)
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthV, 9, 1), -0.002 / 2)
            'Moment U
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthU, 4, 3), 0.002 / 2)
            CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthV, 4, 2), 0, 0.000000000000001)
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthU, 10, 1), 0.002 / 2)
            CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthV, 10, 3), 0, 0.000000000000001)
            'Moment UV. Half the applied moment because there are two of them - UV and VU.
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthUV, 13, 1), -0.002 / 2 / 2)
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthUV, 14, 2), -0.002 / 2 / 2)
        End If


        For Each min4t As Boolean In {False, True}

            'Tests fix of Mystran bug with stress and force per length with orthotropic CTE on shells.
            If SolveFile("NAS S30 shell orthotropic thermal expansion stress and force.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
                Dim yStress As Double = -200000000000.0 * 0.0025 * 3 'stress = -E * CTE * deltaT
                Messages.AppendLine("Stress VV")
                For Each elemId As Integer In {25, 37, 42, 1, 20, 47}
                    CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressVV), elemId, 1), yStress, 0.0004)
                Next
                Messages.AppendLine("Stress UU,UV")
                For Each elemId As Integer In {25, 37, 42, 1, 20, 47}
                    'Error is rounding error in the low precision .F06 output propogating through the transformation from element to material coordinates.
                    CompareDifference(ElementValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressUU), elemId, 2), 0, 600)
                    CompareDifference(ElementValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StressUV), elemId, 3), 0, 600)
                Next
                Messages.AppendLine("Force per unit length V")
                For Each elemId As Integer In {25, 37, 42, 1, 20, 47}
                    CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, elemId, 3), yStress * 0.3)
                Next
                Messages.AppendLine("Force per unit length U, UV")
                For Each elemId As Integer In {25, 37, 42, 1, 20, 47}
                    CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, elemId, 2), 0, 10)
                    CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, elemId, 1), 0, 10)
                Next
            End If

            If SolveFile("NAS S30 shell laminate orthotropic thermal stress.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then

                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 2), 0.0015 * 4)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 3), 0.0015 * 4)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 7), 0.0015 * 4 * 1 / Math.Sqrt(2))
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 6), 0.0015 * 4 * 2 / Math.Sqrt(2))

                If Comlab Then

                    For i As Integer = 1 To 20
                        CompareTrue(False)
                    Next

                Else

                    'Read f06 to get layer stresses because Mecway can't import that without knowing the layer angles.
                    Dim yStress As Double = -200000000000.0 * 0.001 * 4 'stress = -E * CTE * deltaT = -8.0e8

                    Dim f06Name As String
                    If ReadWriteRead Then
                        f06Name = "testsave.f06"
                    Else
                        f06Name = "NAS_S30_shell_laminate_orthotropic_thermal_stress.f06"
                    End If
                    Dim f06contents As String() = IO.File.ReadAllLines(TempFolder & "mystran" & "/" & f06Name)
                    Dim s11 As Double
                    Dim s22 As Double
                    Dim s12 As Double
                    Dim position As Integer = 0

                    FindLine("S T R E S S E S   I N   L A Y E R E D   C O M P O S I T E   E L E M E N T S", f06contents, True, position)
                    FindLine("F O R   E L E M E N T   T Y P E   Q U A D 4", f06contents, True, position)

                    'CQUAD4 Ply 1
                    Dim q4Ply1Line As String = FindLine("3     1", f06contents, True, position)
                    s11 = ValDbl(q4Ply1Line.Substring(17 - 1, 12))
                    s22 = ValDbl(q4Ply1Line.Substring(30 - 1, 12))
                    s12 = ValDbl(q4Ply1Line.Substring(43 - 1, 12))
                    CompareRatioSingle(s11, yStress)
                    CompareDifference(s22, 0, 0.000001)
                    CompareDifference(s12, 0, 1)

                    'CQUAD4 Ply 2
                    Dim q4Ply2Line As String = FindLine("2 ", f06contents, True, position)
                    s11 = ValDbl(q4Ply2Line.Substring(17 - 1, 12))
                    s22 = ValDbl(q4Ply2Line.Substring(30 - 1, 12))
                    s12 = ValDbl(q4Ply2Line.Substring(43 - 1, 12))
                    CompareRatioSingle(s11, yStress)
                    CompareDifference(s22, 0, 0.000001)
                    CompareDifference(s12, 0, 1)

                    FindLine("S T R E S S E S   I N   L A Y E R E D   C O M P O S I T E   E L E M E N T S", f06contents, True, position)
                    FindLine("F O R   E L E M E N T   T Y P E   T R I A 3", f06contents, True, position)

                    'CTRIA3 element 1 Ply 1
                    Dim t31Ply1Line As String = FindLine("1     1", f06contents, True, position)
                    s11 = ValDbl(t31Ply1Line.Substring(17 - 1, 12))
                    s22 = ValDbl(t31Ply1Line.Substring(30 - 1, 12))
                    s12 = ValDbl(t31Ply1Line.Substring(43 - 1, 12))
                    CompareRatioSingle(s11, yStress)
                    CompareDifference(s22, 0, 0.000001)
                    CompareDifference(s12, 0, 0.000001)

                    'CTRIA3 element 2 Ply 2
                    Dim t32Ply2Line As String = FindLine("2     1", f06contents, True, position)
                    s11 = ValDbl(t32Ply2Line.Substring(17 - 1, 12))
                    s22 = ValDbl(t32Ply2Line.Substring(30 - 1, 12))
                    s12 = ValDbl(t32Ply2Line.Substring(43 - 1, 12))
                    CompareRatioSingle(s11, yStress)
                    CompareDifference(s22, 0, 0.000001)
                    CompareDifference(s12, 0, 0.000001)

                End If

            End If

        Next

        'Isotropic and same properties for each layer.
        If SolveFile("NAS B30 quad4 laminate isotropic homogenous.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Reference solution from equivalent isotropic non-laminate material and internal solver refined quad8
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), 4135.352) 'Internal solver 4111
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(2), 25828.08) 'Internal solver 25660
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(3), 38943.34) 'Internal solver 36910
            CompareDifference(AngleFrom({4}, 1, AxisType.Z), 0)
            CompareDifference(AngleFrom({4, 12}, 2, AxisType.X), 0)
            CompareDifference(AngleFrom({4}, 3, AxisType.Z), 0)
        End If


        'Isotropic and different properties for each layer.
        If SolveFile("NAS B30 quad4 laminate isotropic.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Reference solution from CCX refined quadratic elements. High error because of coarse mesh.
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), 5653.814, 0.7)
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(2), 50827.98, 6)
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(3), 64144.35, 0.8)
            CompareDifference(AngleFrom({4}, 1, AxisType.Z), 0)
            CompareDifference(AngleFrom({4}, 2, AxisType.Z), 0)
            CompareDifference(AngleFrom({4, 12}, 3, AxisType.X), 0)
        End If

        If SolveFile("NAS B30 quad4 laminate isotropic refined.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Making sure NAS B30 quad4 laminate isotropic.liml converges with refinement.
            'Reference solution from CCX refined quadratic elements.
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), 5653.814, 0.05)
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(2), 50827.98, 5)
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(3), 64144.35, 0.05)
            CompareDifference(AngleFrom({4}, 1, AxisType.Z), 0)
            CompareDifference(AngleFrom({4}, 2, AxisType.Z), 0)
            CompareDifference(AngleFrom({4, 12}, 3, AxisType.X), 0)
        End If

        'Orthotropic and different properties for each layer.
        'Also some inverted elements which should have no effect because the laminate is symmetric.
        If SolveFile("NAS B30 quad4 laminate axis aligned.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Reference solution from CCX refined quadratic elements. High error because of coarse mesh.
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), 9251.055, 0.2)
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(2), 76954.37, 0.3)
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(3), 83120.7, 2)
            CompareDifference(AngleFrom({4}, 1, AxisType.Z), 0)
            CompareDifference(AngleFrom({4, 12}, 2, AxisType.X), 0)
            CompareDifference(AngleFrom({4}, 3, AxisType.Z), 0)
        End If


        'When Mystran won't allow non-symmetric composites, don't run these tests even though they're effectively symmetric and would otherwise pass.
        If False Then

            'Orthotropic and same properties for each layer.
            If SolveFile("NAS B30 quad4 laminate oriented homogenous.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
                'Reference solution from equivalent orthotropic non-laminate material and internal solver refined quad8
                CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), 8264.919) 'Internal solver 8220
                CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(2), 51602.87) 'Internal solver 51250
                CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(3), 77375.05) 'Internal solver 73610
                CompareDifference(AngleFrom({4}, 1, AxisType.Z), 0)
                CompareDifference(AngleFrom({4, 12}, 2, AxisType.X), 0)
                CompareDifference(AngleFrom({4}, 3, AxisType.Z), 0)
            End If

            'Orthotropic and different properties for each layer. Also rotated layers and material coordinates.
            If SolveFile("NAS B30 quad4 laminate oriented.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
                'Reference solution is NAS B30 quad4 laminate axis aligned.liml unrefined to the same mesh, which should have identical results.
                CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), 9301.75)
                CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(2), 77468.23)
                CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(3), 87374.39)
                CompareDifference(AngleFrom({4}, 1, AxisType.Z), 0)
                CompareDifference(AngleFrom({4, 12}, 2, AxisType.X), 0)
                CompareDifference(AngleFrom({4}, 3, AxisType.Z), 0)
            End If
        Else
            For i As Integer = 1 To 14
                CompareTrue(True)
            Next
        End If

        'Deformed elements
        'Various layer angles
        'Plate buckling
        If SolveFile("NAS B30 quad4 laminate 8 layer plate deformed.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Reference solution from Structural FEA "PlateMesh Validation Cases v1.1", Composite, Case 2.
            '   Classical solution: 56.8
            '   FEA: 54.3
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), 54.3, 3)
        End If

        Messages.Append("Number of tests")
        CompareDifference(TestCount - initialTestCount, 254)
        functionCount += 1

    End Sub '"

    Private Sub Nastran4(testFilesFolder As String, ByRef functionCount As Integer)
        Dim initialTestCount As Integer = TestCount

        Dim model As ModelData

        If SolveFile("NAS S30 bar BANDED BAILOUT -1.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'BANDED can't solve this even with bailout turned off. It's OK if it can solve it oneday.
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 4), NoValue)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 4), NoValue)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4), NoValue)
            'It should produce a 0 solution at the constraints.
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementMag, 1), 0)
        End If

        If SolveFile("NAS S30 bar BANDED BAILOUT default.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Should have no solution because BAILOUT stops it.
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 4), NoValue)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 4), NoValue)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4), NoValue)
            'Should have no solution at the constrained node because BAILOUT stops it.
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 1), NoValue)
        End If

        If SolveFile("NAS S30 bar SPARSE BAILOUT -1.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'SPARSE can solve it with BAILOUT turned off.
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 4), 0.0000381971863420549)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 4), 0)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4), 0)
            'It should produce a 0 solution at the constraints.
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementMag, 1), 0)
        End If

        If SolveFile("NAS S30 bar SPARSE BAILOUT default.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Should have no solution because BAILOUT stops it.
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 4), NoValue)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 4), NoValue)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4), NoValue)
            'Should have no solution at the constrained node because BAILOUT stops it.
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 1), NoValue)
        End If

        If SolveFile("NAS S30 solid BANDED BAILOUT -1.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'BANDED can solve it with bailout turned off.
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 5), 0.000001)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 7), 0)
        End If

        If SolveFile("NAS S30 solid BANDED BAILOUT default.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Should have no solution because BAILOUT stops it.
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 5), NoValue)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 7), NoValue)
        End If

        If SolveFile("NAS S30 solid SPARSE BAILOUT -1.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'SPARSE can solve it with bailout turned off.
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 5), 0.000001)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 7), 0)
        End If

        If SolveFile("NAS S30 solid SPARSE BAILOUT default.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Should have no solution because BAILOUT stops it.
            'However, since the BAILOUT ratio test isn't currently being done with SPARSE, it does solve.
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 5), 0.000001)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 7), 0)
        End If

        'Make sure it doesn't bend due to greater area on the outside of the curve.
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 shell pressure curved edge.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            Dim u As Double = 10000000.0 * 0.3 / 200000000000.0
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 18), u, 0.06)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 31), u, 0.06)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 8), u, 0.06)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 12), u / 2, 0.06)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 18), 0, 0.00000001)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 18), 0, 0.00000001)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 16), 0, 0.00000001)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 16), 0, 0.00000001)
        End If

        If SolveFile("NAS S30 3 digit exponents input.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'It will also be OK if it's a fatal error because of the huge E value
            'But it must solve with the tiny node coordinates, either retaining the negative 3-digit exponents
            ' or setting them to zero.
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 8), -1.0E+102 / 1.23412341234123E+103)
        End If

        'Tests stress/strain/force transforms where the element coordinate system is different over the element because it's distorted.
        'Membrane loading only.
        'Straight sided elements.
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 quad8 distorted membrane stress strain force.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            Dim stress As Double = -1230000
            Dim strain As Double = stress / 210000000000
            Dim forcePerLength As Double = stress * 0.22
            'Axis aligned
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {1, 9, 10, 11, 12}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressVV), nodeId), stress, 0.0005)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, nodeId), forcePerLength)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), nodeId), strain, 0.0001)
            Next
            'Rotated 45 deg
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {16, 19, 20, 26}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressVV), nodeId), stress, 0.0005)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, nodeId), forcePerLength)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), strain / 2, 0.0001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), nodeId), strain / 2, 0.0001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXY), nodeId), -strain, 0.0001)
            Next
        End If

        'Tests stress/strain/force/moment transforms where the element coordinate system is different over the element because it's distorted.
        'Bending and shear only
        'Straight sided elements.
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 quad8 distorted bending and shear stress strain force.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            Dim forcePerLength As Double = 12300.0 / 2 / 3
            Dim bendingStress As Double = forcePerLength * 3 * 6 * 0.11 / (3 * 0.22 ^ 3 / 12) 'stress = My/I
            Dim bendingStrain As Double = bendingStress / 210000000000
            Dim momentPerLength As Double = forcePerLength * 6
            Dim shearStress As Double = forcePerLength / 0.22

            'Transverse shear force per unit length
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {1, 9, 10, 11, 12, 16, 19, 20, 26}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthVW, nodeId), forcePerLength, 4)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthWU, nodeId), 0, 30)
            Next
            'Transverse shear stress (may be removed because MSC Nastran doesn't generate this).
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {1, 9, 10, 11, 12, 16, 19, 20, 26}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVW), nodeId), shearStress, 4)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StressVW), nodeId), shearStress, 4)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressWU), nodeId), 0, 200)
            Next
            'Bending stress
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {2, 7, 14, 20}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressVV), nodeId), bendingStress, 0.03)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVV), nodeId), 0)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StressVV), nodeId), -bendingStress, 0.03)
            Next
            'Bending strain
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {1, 2, 7}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), nodeId), bendingStrain, 0.03)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), nodeId), 0)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), nodeId), -bendingStrain, 0.03)
            Next
            'Moment per length
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {1, 2, 7, 14, 15, 20}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthV, nodeId), -momentPerLength, 0.03)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthU, nodeId), 0, 20)
            Next

        End If

        'Tests stress/strain/force transforms where the element coordinate system is different over the element because it's distorted.
        'Membrane loading only.
        'Poisson's ratio
        'Curved sided elements.
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 quad8 distorted midside membrane stress strain force.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            Dim force As Double = 1000000.0
            Dim stress As Double = force / 0.02
            Dim strain As Double = stress / 50000000000
            Dim forcePerLength As Double = stress * 0.02
            Dim lateralStrain As Double = -strain * 0.3
            'Lower error
            For Each elemId As Integer In {1, 2, 6}
                Messages.AppendLine(" Element " & elemId)
                For Each localNode As Integer In {1, 2, 4, 5}
                    CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressUU), elemId, localNode), stress, 0.6)
                    CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, elemId, localNode), forcePerLength, 0.6)
                    CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), elemId, localNode), strain, 0.7)
                    CompareDifference(ElementValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVV), elemId, localNode), 0, 400000)
                    CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, elemId, localNode), 0, 7000)
                    CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), elemId, localNode), lateralStrain, 3)
                Next
            Next
            'Higher error for some reason
            For Each elemId As Integer In {7}
                Messages.AppendLine(" Element " & elemId)
                For Each localNode As Integer In {1, 2, 4, 5}
                    CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressUU), elemId, localNode), stress, 4)
                    CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, elemId, localNode), forcePerLength, 4)
                    CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), elemId, localNode), strain, 4)
                    CompareDifference(ElementValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVV), elemId, localNode), 0, 700000)
                    CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, elemId, localNode), 0, 20000)
                    CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), elemId, localNode), lateralStrain, 8)
                Next
            Next
        End If

        'Flat elements distorted in-plane
        'Test that node number ordering doesn't affect the solution
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 quad8 distorted node numbering invariance inplane.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            For Each refNode As Integer In {1, 3}
                For Each nodeId As Integer In If(refNode = 1, {9, 17, 25}, {11, 19, 27})
                    Messages.AppendLine(" Nodes " & refNode & " and " & nodeId)

                    CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, refNode))
                    CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, refNode))
                    CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, refNode))
                    CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationX, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationX, refNode))
                    CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, refNode))
                    CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, refNode))

                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressUU), nodeId), NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressUU), refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVV), nodeId), NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVV), refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StressUV), nodeId), NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StressUV), refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressVW), nodeId), NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressVW), refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressWU), nodeId), NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressWU), refNode))

                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), nodeId), NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainXY), nodeId), NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainXY), refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainYZ), nodeId), NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainYZ), refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainZX), nodeId), NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainZX), refNode))

                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthVW, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthVW, refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthWU, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthWU, refNode))

                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthU, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthU, refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthV, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthV, refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthUV, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthUV, refNode))

                Next
            Next
        End If

        'Square elements curved out-of-plane
        'Test that node number ordering doesn't affect the solution
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 quad8 distorted node numbering invariance curved.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            For Each refNode As Integer In {1, 3}
                For Each nodeId As Integer In If(refNode = 1, {9, 17, 25}, {11, 19, 27})
                    Messages.AppendLine(" Nodes " & refNode & " and " & nodeId)

                    CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, refNode))
                    CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, refNode))
                    CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, refNode))
                    CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationX, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationX, refNode))
                    CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, refNode))
                    CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, refNode))

                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressUU), nodeId), NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressUU), refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVV), nodeId), NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVV), refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StressUV), nodeId), NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StressUV), refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressVW), nodeId), NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressVW), refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressWU), nodeId), NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressWU), refNode))

                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), nodeId), NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainXY), nodeId), NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainXY), refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainYZ), nodeId), NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainYZ), refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainZX), nodeId), NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainZX), refNode))

                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthVW, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthVW, refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthWU, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthWU, refNode))

                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthU, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthU, refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthV, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthV, refNode))
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthUV, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthUV, refNode))

                Next
            Next
        End If

        'Square but midside nodes distorted out-of-plane equally at each midside node so it's rotationally symmetric
        'Test that node number ordering doesn't affect the solution
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 quad8 distorted node numbering invariance midside outofplane.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            For Each refNode As Integer In {1, 3}
                For Each nodeId As Integer In If(refNode = 1, {9}, {11})
                    Messages.AppendLine(" Nodes " & refNode & " and " & nodeId)

                    CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, refNode))
                    CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, refNode))
                    CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, refNode))
                    CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationX, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationX, refNode))
                    CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, refNode))
                    CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, refNode))

                    'These have big error but there may be asymmetry in the definitions of the coordinate systems so perhaps it's OK. Not sure.
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StressUV), nodeId), NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StressUV), refNode), 30)
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainXY), nodeId), NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainXY), refNode), 40)
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, refNode), 60)
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthU, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthU, refNode), 30)
                Next
            Next
        End If


        'Elements distorted in all ways
        'Test that node number ordering doesn't affect the solution
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 quad8 distorted node numbering invariance 3D.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            For Each refNode As Integer In {1, 3}
                For Each nodeId As Integer In If(refNode = 1, {9, 17, 25}, {11, 19, 27})
                    Messages.AppendLine(" Nodes " & refNode & " and " & nodeId)

                    CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, refNode))
                    CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, refNode))
                    CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, refNode))
                    CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationX, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationX, refNode))
                    CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, refNode))
                    CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, refNode))

                    'These have big error but there may be asymmetry in the definitions of the coordinate systems so perhaps it's OK. Not sure.
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StressUV), nodeId), NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StressUV), refNode), 80)
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainXY), nodeId), NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainXY), refNode), 30)
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, refNode), 30)
                    CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthU, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthU, refNode), 3)

                Next
            Next
        End If

        'Elements singley curved about different axes
        'Uniaxial stress
        'Stress, strain, force per length
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 quad8 cylinder distorted.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            Dim forcePerLength As Double = 1000000.0 / (Math.PI / 2)
            Dim stress As Double = forcePerLength / 0.01
            Dim strain As Double = stress / 200000000000.0
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {9, 20, 70, 79, 92, 123}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, nodeId), forcePerLength, 0.5)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressUU), nodeId), stress, 4)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressUU), nodeId), stress, 0.5)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StressUU), nodeId), stress, 4)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainZZ), nodeId), strain, 3)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainZZ), nodeId), strain, 0.5)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainZZ), nodeId), strain, 3)
            Next
        End If

        'Unsupported CQUAD8 features
        For Each fileName As String In {"quad8 no 12itm3.liml",
                                        "quad8 no buckling.liml",
                                        "quad8 no gravity.liml",
                                        "quad8 no mat8.liml",
                                        "quad8 no mid3 ne mid2.liml",
                                        "quad8 no mid4.liml",
                                        "quad8 no nsm.liml",
                                        "quad8 no orthotopic.liml",
                                        "quad8 no pressure.liml",
                                        "quad8 no sol 3.liml",
                                        "quad8 no T1-T4.liml",
                                        "quad8 no thermal strain.liml",
                                        "quad8 no z1 z2.liml",
                                        "quad8 no zoffs.liml"}
            SolveFile(fileName, testFilesFolder & "/NAS CQUAD8 unsupported features", model)

            If model Is Nothing Then
                'Might be an error reading the truncated .f06. Might be acceptable.
                CompareDifference(1, 2) 'Force fail that's distinct from the model isnot nothing fails below.
                CompareDifference(1, 2)
            Else
                'Make sure it tried to solve (opened file, ran solver, copied solution mesh in).
                CompareTrue(model.NumNodes > 0)
                'Make sure there's no solution data because the feature isn't supported so Mystran shouldn't silently ignore it.
                CompareTrue(model.FieldVariables.NodeValueNullable(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 1) Is Nothing)
            End If

        Next


        Messages.Append("Number of tests")
        CompareDifference(TestCount - initialTestCount, 712)
        functionCount += 1

    End Sub '"

    Private Sub Nastran5Mitc8(testFilesFolder As String, ByRef functionCount As Integer)

        'Same as the internal solver's MITC8 tests but with some looser tolerances because of number formatting in solver input and output files.

        'Mostly the same tests for MITC3, MITC4, MITC6, MITC8
        '
        '# means it has a test case
        '- means it doesn't have or need a test case
        '                         Simple cases    Non-rectangular     Non-planar
        '   Membrane displacement #               -                   # MacNeal
        '   Bending displacement                  #                   # MacNeal
        '   Reaction force        -               -                   #
        '   Reaction moment       #               #                   #
        '   Strain                #               # MacNeal
        '   Stress                                # MacNeal
        '   Force per length      #
        '   Moment per length     #               # MacNeal
        '   Drilling spring       #               -
        '   Orthotropic           #               #                   
        '       membrane          -               #                   
        '       bending/shear     #               -                   
        '   Laminate              #               #                   #
        '       displacement      -               #
        '       stress            -               #
        '       force per length  #               #
        '       moment per length -               #
        '       reaction force    -               -                   
        '       reaction moment   -               #
        '       drilling spring   #               -                   -
        '   Offset shell                                              #
        '   Thermal stress        -               -                   -
        '       Free expansion    #               #                   #
        '       Orthotropic       #               #                   #
        '       Uniform temp      #               #                   # 
        '       Strain            #               #                   #
        '       Stress            #               -                   -
        '       Linear temp       -               #                   -
        '       Quadratic temp    -               #                   -
        '       Constrained       #               #                   -
        '       Offset            #               -                   #
        '   Buckling              -
        '       basic             -               #                   #
        '       thermal stress    #
        '   Eigen                 -               -                   #
        '       Spin softening    -               -                   #
        '       Stress stiffening -               -                   #
        '          Thermal stress -               -                   -
        '   Dynamic response      -               #                   #
        '   Modal response        -               #                   -
        '   Edge loads using non-flat geometry
        '   Face loads using non-flat geometry
        '   Body loads using non-flat geometry

        Dim initialTestCount As Integer = TestCount

        Dim model As ModelData

        Dim solver As SolverType = SolverType.Mystran

        'Displacement, stress for the simplest loading.
        If SolveFile("S30 mitc8 flat membrane normal force.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Stress = 50 MPa
            'Strain  = 50MPa / 200 GPa = 0.00025
            'Displacement = 0.0025 * 0.03 = 7.5e-6
            'Transverse strain = nu * strain = 0.4 * 0.00025 = 0.0001
            'Transverse displacement = 0.021 * 0.0001 = 2.1e-6
            'Reaction force = 50MPa * 1e-3 * 0.021 = 1050 N

            'Displacement
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {2, 3, 6, 7, 10, 11, 14, 15}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, nodeId), 0.0000075)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, nodeId), 0, 0.0000000000000001)
            Next
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {34, 38, 42, 46}
                'A little error probably because it's calculating the magnitudes from the low precision components in the f06 file.
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementMag, nodeId), 0.0000075, 0.0001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, nodeId), 0, 0.0000000000000001)
            Next
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {18, 22, 26, 30, 19, 23, 27, 31}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, nodeId), 0.0000075)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, nodeId), 0, 0.0000000000000001)
            Next
            'Node rotation
            Messages.Append("Node rotation" + vbNewLine)
            Dim sum_squares As Double = 0
            For nodeId As Integer = 1 To 48
                sum_squares += NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationX, nodeId) ^ 2
                sum_squares += NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, nodeId) ^ 2
                sum_squares += NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, nodeId) ^ 2
            Next
            CompareDifference(Math.Sqrt(sum_squares), 0, 0.00000000000001)

            'Smaller error using MKL dgemm for BTDB
            'CompareDifference(max, 0, 0.00000000000001)
            'Transverse displacement
            Messages.Append("Transverse displacement" + vbNewLine)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {3, 7, 11, 15, 31, 27, 23, 19}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, nodeId), -0.0000021)
            Next
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {35, 39, 43, 47}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementMag, nodeId), Math.Sqrt(0.0000021 ^ 2 + 0.0000075 ^ 2))
            Next
            'Stress
            Messages.Append("Stress" + vbNewLine)
            Messages.Append(" U" + vbNewLine)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {1, 2, 9, 11, 34, 35, 41, 44, 30, 32, 21, 22}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressUU), nodeId), 50000000.0)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVV), nodeId), 0, 0.01)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressUV), nodeId), 0, 0.03)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVW), nodeId), 0, 0.00001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressWU), nodeId), 0, 0.00001)
            Next
            Messages.Append(" V" + vbNewLine)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {5, 6, 13, 14, 40, 39, 48, 47, 19, 20, 27, 28}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVV), nodeId), 50000000.0)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressUU), nodeId), 0, 0.01)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressUV), nodeId), 0, 0.03)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVW), nodeId), 0, 0.00001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressWU), nodeId), 0, 0.00001)
            Next

        End If

        'Force per length all components only for simple shapes.
        If SolveFile("S30 mitc8 force per length.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            Messages.Append(" U" + vbNewLine)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {1, 2, 18, 19}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, nodeId), 3000000 / 2 / 1.7)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, nodeId), 0, 0.000000002)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthVW, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthWU, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
            Next
            Messages.Append(" UV" + vbNewLine)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {21, 22, 7, 8}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, nodeId), 3000000 / 2 / 1.7 / 2)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, nodeId), 3000000 / 2 / 1.7 / 2)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, nodeId), -3000000 / 2 / 1.7 / 2)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthVW, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthWU, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
            Next
            Messages.Append(" UW" + vbNewLine)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {9, 25, 26, 12}
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthVW, nodeId), 0, 0.00000001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthWU, nodeId), 0.003 / 2 / 0.8, 0.006)
            Next
            Messages.Append(" VW" + vbNewLine)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {32, 29, 15, 16}
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthVW, nodeId), -0.003 / 2 / 0.8, 0.008)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthWU, nodeId), 0, 0.00000001)
            Next
        End If


        'Reaction force on arbitrary shaped mesh.
        If SolveFile("S30 mitc8 reaction force.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            Dim forceSum As New VectorDouble
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {2, 6, 7, 8}
                forceSum.X += NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionForceX, nodeId)
                forceSum.Y += NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionForceY, nodeId)
                forceSum.Z += NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionForceZ, nodeId)
            Next
            'Small error may be due to coordinate transform on one of the nodes.
            CompareRatioSingle(forceSum.X, -10, 0.0005)
            CompareRatioSingle(forceSum.Y, -20, 0.00005)
            CompareRatioSingle(forceSum.Z, -30, 0.0003)
        End If

        'Reaction moment on rectangular elements with zero prescribed rotation at supports.
        If SolveFile("S30 mitc8 reaction moment easy.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            Dim forceSum As New VectorDouble
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {1, 2, 5, 6}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentX, nodeId), -1.1)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentY, nodeId), 0, 0.0000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentZ, nodeId), 0, 0.0000000001)
            Next
        End If

        'Reaction moment with non-rectangular shape and multiple elements but still flat
        If SolveFile("S30 mitc8 reaction moment nonzero rotation.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Moment doesn't have to be split -0.35, -0.35, -1.5 but the sum should be -2.2
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {5, 6}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentX, nodeId), -0.35, 0.2)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentY, nodeId), 0, 0.0000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentZ, nodeId), 0, 0.0000000001)
            Next
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentX, 21), -1.5, 0.1)
            Dim sum As Double = NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentX, 1) +
                                NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentX, 2) +
                                NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentX, 9) +
                                NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentX, 22) +
                                NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentX, 25)
            CompareRatioSingle(sum, -2.2, 0.0003)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {1, 9, 22}
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentY, nodeId), 0, 0.0000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentZ, nodeId), 0, 0.0000000001)
            Next
        End If

        If SolveFile("S30 mitc8 reaction moment complex shape.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Covariant basis z direction interpolated director vectors at nodes.
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentX, 8), -1.1, 0.0004)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentY, 7), -2.2, 0.0001)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentZ, 6), -3.3, 0.0001)
        End If

        'Strain for simple square shapes
        If SolveFile("S30 mitc8 strain.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            Messages.Append(" XX" + vbNewLine)
            'XX
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {9, 39}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), 0.13 / 1.2, 0.0005)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), 0.13 / 1.2, 0.0005)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), 0.13 / 1.2, 0.0005)
            Next
            'XX bending strain 
            'Stress = My/I. M=0.006. y=+/-0.15. I=1.2*0.3^3/12 = 0.333333
            'E=stress/strain. Strain = 0.066666
            Messages.Append(" XX bending" + vbNewLine)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {21, 22, 52, 49}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), -0.06666666666666, 0.0001)
