                CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), 0, 0.00000000001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), 0.06666666666666, 0.0001)
            Next
            'YY
            Messages.Append(" YY" + vbNewLine)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {13, 42}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), nodeId), 0.13 / 1.2, 0.0005)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), nodeId), 0.13 / 1.2, 0.0005)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), nodeId), 0.13 / 1.2, 0.0005)
            Next
            'XY shear strain
            Messages.Append(" XY" + vbNewLine)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {29, 30, 3, 4}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXY), nodeId), 0.012 / 1.2)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainXY), nodeId), 0.012 / 1.2)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainXY), nodeId), 0.012 / 1.2)
            Next
            'YZ shear strain. It's constant through the thickness because of shell idealization.
            Messages.Append(" YZ" + vbNewLine)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {17, 19, 45, 46}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainYZ), nodeId), 0.1 / 1.2, 0.00005)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainYZ), nodeId), 0.1 / 1.2, 0.00005)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainYZ), nodeId), 0.1 / 1.2, 0.00005)
            Next
            'ZX shear strain. It's constant through the thickness because of shell idealization.
            Messages.Append(" ZX" + vbNewLine)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {36, 6, 35, 8}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainZX), nodeId), 0.1 / 1.2, 0.00005)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainZX), nodeId), 0.1 / 1.2, 0.00005)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainZX), nodeId), 0.1 / 1.2, 0.00005)
            Next
            'Shear strain rotated with factor of 2 used correctly
            'In element coordinates : gamma_UU = 0, VV=0, UV=0.07/1.2.
            Messages.Append(" XX,YY,XY rotated" + vbNewLine)
            Dim s As Double = Math.Sin(2 * -20 * Math.PI / 180)
            Dim c As Double = Math.Cos(2 * -20 * Math.PI / 180)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {25, 26, 27, 56}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), 0.07 / 1.2 / 2 * s, 0.0001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), nodeId), -0.07 / 1.2 / 2 * s, 0.0001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXY), nodeId), (0.07 / 1.2 / 2 * c) * 2, 0.0001)
            Next

        End If

        'Excluded because not supported by Mystran  "S30 mitc8 drilling stiffness.liml"

        If SolveFile("S30 mitc8 macneal membrane patch test.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Reference solution from MacNeal and Harder "A proposed standard set of problems to test finite element accuracy":
            'strain_xx = 1e-3
            'strain_yy = 1e-3
            'strain_xy = 1e-3 (gamma, engineering shear strain)
            'stress_xx = 1333 Pa
            'stress_yy = 1333 Pa
            'stress_xy = 400 Pa

            For Each nodeId As Integer In {2, 3, 6, 7, 8, 12, 15, 17}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), 0.001, 0.0003)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), 0.001, 0.0003)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), 0.001, 0.0003)

                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), nodeId), 0.001, 0.0003)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), nodeId), 0.001, 0.0003)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), nodeId), 0.001, 0.0003)

                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXY), nodeId), 0.001, 0.0004)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainXY), nodeId), 0.001, 0.0004)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainXY), nodeId), 0.001, 0.0004)

                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressUU), nodeId), 1333, 0.04)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVV), nodeId), 1333, 0.04)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressUV), nodeId), 400, 0.0005)
            Next

            'Stress UU bottom and top
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressUU), 1, 1), 1333, 0.04)
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StressUU), 1, 1), 1333, 0.04)
            'Stress VV bottom and top
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressVV), 1, 1), 1333, 0.04)
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StressVV), 1, 1), 1333, 0.04)
            'Stress UV bottom and top
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressUV), 1, 1), 400, 0.00005)
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StressUV), 1, 1), 400, 0.00005)
        End If


        If SolveFile("S30 mitc8 macneal plate bending patch test.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Reference solution from MacNeal and Harder "A proposed standard set of problems to test finite element accuracy":
            'Bending moment per unit length x = 1.111e-7 N.m/m
            'Bending moment per unit length y = 1.111e-7 N.m/m but that looks like a mistake and it should be 3.33e-8 which
            'matches the analytic integral of stress_xy * z over the thickness.
            'Bending moment per unit length xy = 1e-7 N.m/m
            'stress xx = +/- 0.667 Pa
            'stress yy = +/- 0.667 Pa
            'stress xy = +/- 0.200 Pa

            For Each nodeId As Integer In {5, 8, 9, 10, 12, 13, 14, 15}
                'Sign convention of moments in Mecway is opposite MacNeal so these are negative.
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthU, nodeId), -0.0000001111, 0.02)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthV, nodeId), -0.0000001111, 0.02)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthUV, nodeId), -0.0000000333, 0.2)
                'Stress UU bottom and top
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressUU), nodeId), 0.667, 0.06)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StressUU), nodeId), -0.667, 0.06)
                'Stress VV bottom and top
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressVV), nodeId), 0.667, 0.06)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StressVV), nodeId), -0.667, 0.06)
                'Stress UV bottom and top
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressUV), nodeId), 0.2, 0.0002)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StressUV), nodeId), -0.2, 0.0002)
            Next
        End If

        'Simple rectangles
        If SolveFile("S30 mitc8 moment per length.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'U (about V)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {5, 21, 22, 7}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthU, nodeId), 1.1 / 0.8)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthV, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthUV, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
            Next
            'V (about U)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {18, 19, 4, 1}
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthU, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthV, nodeId), 1.1 / 0.8)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthUV, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
            Next
            'UV
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {9, 28, 12, 27}
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthU, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthV, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthUV, nodeId), -3.3 * 0.8 / 0.8 / 2)
            Next
            'Combined with rotated axes
            'All 3 should be positive.
            'U And V should be equal by symmetry.
            'All 3 should be equal to half the applied moment because a uniaxial normal stress rotated by 45 degrees becomes x,y,xy stresses each of half the value.
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {13, 29, 32, 15}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthU, nodeId), 1.1 / 0.8 / 2)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthV, nodeId), 1.1 / 0.8 / 2)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthUV, nodeId), 1.1 / 0.8 / 2)
            Next
        End If


        'Excluded because not supported by Mystran "S30 mitc8 orthotropic membrane.liml"

        'Excluded because not supported by Mystran "S30 mitc8 orthotropic shear.liml"


        If SolveFile("S30 mitc8 macneal twisted beam.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Reference displacement solutions from MacNeal and Harder "A proposed standard set of problems to test finite element accuracy".

            Project.Configurations.SetActiveConfiguration(Project.Configurations.GetByName("In-plane"))
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 35), 0.005424, 0.3)
            Dim reactionSum As Double = 0
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {1, 14, 27, 40, 77}
                reactionSum += NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentY, nodeId)
            Next
            CompareRatioSingle(reactionSum, -12, 0.00001)

            Project.Configurations.SetActiveConfiguration(Project.Configurations.GetByName("Out-of-plane"))
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 35), 0.001754, 0.4)
            reactionSum = 0
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {1, 14, 27, 40, 77}
                reactionSum += NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentY, nodeId)
            Next
            CompareDifference(reactionSum, 0, 0.000002) 'Must be << 12
        End If


        If SolveFile("S30 mitc8 MacNeal spherical shell.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Reference solutions from MacNeal and Harder "A proposed standard set of problems to test finite element accuracy".
            'Can also be 0.0924 with the center hole filled.
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 12), -0.094, 3)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 1), 0.094, 3)
        End If

        'Shows no serious membrane locking which is overstiffness for thin curved elements.
        'Skewed curved elements.
        If SolveFile("S30 mitc8 helical mesh ring.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Reference solution taken from meshes with non-skewed elements.
            Dim reference As Double = 0.017
            'Coarse mesh
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 2), reference, 50)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 712), reference, 50)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 18), 0, 0.003)
            'Fine mesh
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 595), reference, 0.1)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 840), reference, 0.1)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 834), 0, 0.0002)
        End If

        'Excluded because not supported by Mystran "S30 mitc8 laminate membrane symmetry.liml"

        'Excluded because not supported by Mystran "S30 mitc8 laminate moment per length.liml"

        'Excluded because not supported by Mystran "S30 mitc8 laminate shear.liml"

        'Excluded because not supported by Mystran "S30 mitc8 laminate arbitrary shape.liml"

        'Excluded because not supported by Mystran "S30 mitc8 thermal stress free expansion.liml"

        'Excluded because not supported by Mystran "S30 mitc8 thermal strain free rectangle cylinder.liml"

        'Excluded because not supported by Mystran "S30 mitc8 thermal strain free diamond cylinder.liml"

        'Excluded because not supported by Mystran "S30 mitc8 thermal stress linear temperature.liml"

        'Excluded because not supported by Mystran "S30 mitc8 thermal stress quadratic temperature.liml"

        'Excluded because not supported by Mystran "S30 mitc8 thermal stress linear temperature constrained.liml"

        'Excluded because not supported by Mystran "S30 mitc8 thermal stress linear temperature constrained deformed.liml"

        'Excluded because not supported by Mystran "S30 mitc8 thermal stress offset.liml"

        'Excluded because not supported by Mystran "S30 mitc8 thermal stress offset curved.liml"

        'Excluded because not supported by Mystran "B30 mitc8 cylinder.liml"

        'Excluded because not supported by Mystran "B30 mitc8 cylinder deformed.liml"

        'Excluded because not supported by Mystran "B30 mitc8 thermal stress.liml"

        'Excluded because not supported by Mystran "V30 mitc8 artificial node rotational inertias.liml"

        'Excluded because not supported by Mystran "V30 mitc8 spin softening.liml"

        'Excluded because not supported by Mystran "D30 mitc8 flat deformed.liml"

        'Excluded because not supported by Mystran "D30 mitc8 flat deformed mode superposition.liml"

        'Excluded because not supported by Mystran "D30 mitc8 Raasch hook.liml"

        'Special test for MITC8
        'Rotating node numbering should make no difference. Checks the g_rs/g_rr term in the in-layer-strain.
        If SolveFile("S30 mitc8  skewed membrane RS symmetry.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            CompareTrue(model.FieldVariables.NodeValueNullable(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVonMises), 8).HasValue)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVonMises), 8),
                               NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVonMises), 16), 0)
        End If



        If SolveFile("S30 mitc8 extreme high x low E.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {5, 7, 9, 10, 16}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressUU), nodeId), 0.04, 0.0002)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVV), nodeId), 0, 0.00000001)
            Next
        End If

        'Excluded because not supported by Mystran "S30 mitc8 extreme high E low x.liml"

        Messages.Append("Number of tests")
        CompareDifference(TestCount - initialTestCount, 618)

        functionCount += 1

    End Sub '"

    Private Sub Nastran6(testFilesFolder As String, ByRef functionCount As Integer)
        Dim initialTestCount As Integer = TestCount

        Dim model As ModelData

        If SolveFile("NAS S30 quad4 mitc4 flat.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Reference values from internal solver.
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 7), 0.0000182749435754551, 0.00005)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 7), 0.000000015943383135542, 0.00005)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 7), 0.000190535319497389)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationX, 5), 0.000108108589526862)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, 5), -0.00001853936142126)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 5), 0.00000552753129589125, 0.00005)
        End If

        If SolveFile("NAS S30 quad4 mitc4+ flat.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Reference values from internal solver.
            'It should be identical to MITC4 because all elements are planar.
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 7), 0.0000182749435754551, 0.00005)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 7), 0.000000015943383135542, 0.00005)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 7), 0.000190535319497389)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationX, 5), 0.000108108589526862)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, 5), -0.00001853936142126)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 5), 0.00000552753129589125, 0.00005)
        End If

        If SolveFile("NAS S30 quad4 mitc4+ twisted.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Coarse mesh
            'Reference values from existing solution to detect unexpected changes.
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 6), 0.0009810526)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 6), 0.0003548482)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 6), -0.002456436)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationX, 4), -0.003779204)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, 4), -0.003106242)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 4), 0.002727718)
            'Refined mesh
            'Reference values from even further refined mesh and corroborated with MITC8 in Mystran and internal solver.
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 12), 0.001246482, 5)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 12), 0.001366569, 5)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 12), -0.0007974482, 4)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationX, 10), -0.00107756, 6)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, 10), -0.0002989302, 7)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 10), -0.0004893222, 20)
        End If

        'To identify changes in the coupling between M-B, M-S, and B-S.
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 quad4 mitc4+ twisted thick.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Coarse mesh
            'Reference values from existing solution to detect unexpected changes.
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 6), 0.00002426706)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 6), 0.00001009318)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 6), -0.00005625256)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationX, 4), -0.00008163186)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, 4), -0.00007055677)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 4), 0.00005941908)
            'Refined mesh
            'Reference values from even further refined mesh (2048 elements) with MITC8 in Mystran.
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 12), 0.00004737711, 6)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 12), 0.00003705138, 5)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 12), -0.00003686762, 5)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationX, 10), -0.00002860133, 6)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, 10), -0.00004862711, 10)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 10), -0.00001207394, 4)
        End If

        'Bathe's benchmark for out-of-plane distorted elements where MITC4+ performs much better than MITC4.
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 hemisphere with cutout 4e-3 mitc4.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Reference values from Table 16 in:
            'Performance of the MITC3+ and MITC4+ shell elements in widely-used benchmark problems
            'By Ko,Lee,Lee,Bathe
            '2017
            't/R = 4/1000
            Dim uRef As Double = 0.093
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 1) / uRef, 0.0397, 0.001)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 26) / uRef, 0.1556, 0.005)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 107) / uRef, 0.5815, 0.02)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 396) / uRef, 0.9437, 0.001)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 1485) / uRef, 1.001, 0.05)
        End If

        'Bathe's benchmark for out-of-plane distorted elements where MITC4+ performs much better than MITC4.
        'Without SNORM.
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 hemisphere with cutout 4e-3 mitc4+.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Reference values from Table 16 in:
            'Performance of the MITC3+ and MITC4+ shell elements in widely-used benchmark problems
            'By Ko,Lee,Lee,Bathe
            '2017
            't/R = 4/1000
            Dim uRef As Double = 0.093
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 1) / uRef, 0.8659, 0.1)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 26) / uRef, 0.9736, 0.005)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 107) / uRef, 0.9958, 0.005)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 396) / uRef, 1.003, 0.005)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 1485) / uRef, 1.005, 0.05)
        End If

        'Bathe's benchmark for out-of-plane distorted elements where MITC4+ performs much better than MITC4.
        'Also tests SNORM bulk data entry in Mystran
        'For some reason, SNORM makes it worse at coarser meshes.
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 hemisphere with cutout 4e-3 mitc4+ SNORM.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Reference values from Table 16 in:
            'Performance of the MITC3+ and MITC4+ shell elements in widely-used benchmark problems
            'By Ko,Lee,Lee,Bathe
            '2017
            't/R = 4/1000
            Dim uRef As Double = 0.093
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 1) / uRef, 0.8659, 0.6)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 26) / uRef, 0.9736, 0.2)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 107) / uRef, 0.9958, 0.03)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 396) / uRef, 1.003, 0.01)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 1485) / uRef, 1.005, 0.001)
        End If

        'Bathe's benchmark for out-of-plane distorted elements where MITC4+ performs much better than MITC4.
        'Without SNORM
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 hemisphere with cutout 4e-4 mitc4+.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Reference values from Table 16 in:
            'Performance of the MITC3+ and MITC4+ shell elements in widely-used benchmark problems
            'By Ko,Lee,Lee,Bathe
            '2017
            't/R = 4/10000
            Dim uRef As Double = 0.093
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 1) / uRef, 0.5502, 0.4)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 26) / uRef, 0.9048, 0.1)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 107) / uRef, 0.9736, 0.05)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 396) / uRef, 0.9807, 0.0005)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 1485) / uRef, 0.9831, 0.0005)
        End If

        'Bathe's benchmark for out-of-plane distorted elements where MITC4+ performs much better than MITC4.
        'Also tests SNORM bulk data entry in Mystran
        'For some reason, SNORM makes it worse at coarser meshes.
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 hemisphere with cutout 4e-4 mitc4+ SNORM.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Reference values from Table 16 in:
            'Performance of the MITC3+ and MITC4+ shell elements in widely-used benchmark problems
            'By Ko,Lee,Lee,Bathe
            '2017
            't/R = 4/10000
            Dim uRef As Double = 0.093
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 1) / uRef, 0.5502, 0.6)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 26) / uRef, 0.9048, 0.8)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 107) / uRef, 0.9736, 0.3)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 396) / uRef, 0.9807, 0.01)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 1485) / uRef, 0.9831, 0.005)
        End If

        'Same as NAS S30 hemisphere with cutout 4e-4 mitc4+ SNORM.liml except with K6ROT=100.0
        'Tests that K6ROT is being applied about the correct SNORM axis, not the normal of the flat reference element
        'or the midsurface normal.
        'HOWEVER, it doesn't test that anymore with improved K6ROT (2026-06) which is not sensitive to the normal in this
        'model. Also K6ROT changed from 100 to 1 for less error.
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 hemisphere with cutout 4e-4 mitc4+ SNORM K6ROT.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Reference values from Table 16 in:
            'Performance of the MITC3+ and MITC4+ shell elements in widely-used benchmark problems
            'By Ko,Lee,Lee,Bathe
            '2017
            't/R = 4/10000
            Dim uRef As Double = 0.093
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 1) / uRef, 0.5502, 0.6)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 26) / uRef, 0.9048, 0.8)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 107) / uRef, 0.9736, 0.3)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 396) / uRef, 0.9807, 0.01)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 1485) / uRef, 0.9831, 0.005)
        End If


        If SolveFile("NAS S30 quad4 spatial isotropy mitc4.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {8, 12, 16, 20, 24, 28, 32}
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 4))
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 4))
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4))
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, 4))
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 4))
            Next
            CompareDifference(-NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 36), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 4))
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 36), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 4))
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 36), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4))
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationX, 36), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, 4))
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 36), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 4))
        End If

        If SolveFile("NAS S30 quad4 spatial isotropy mitc4+.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {8, 12, 16, 20, 24, 28, 32}
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 4))
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 4))
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4))
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, 4))
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, nodeId), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 4))
            Next
            CompareDifference(-NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 36), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 4))
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 36), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 4))
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 36), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4))
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationX, 36), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, 4))
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 36), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 4))
        End If

        'Fixed bug adding to KE where quad4 would crash when a quad8 is present and quad8 would crash when a hex20 was present.
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 quad4 quad8 hex20 together.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            Dim strain As Double = (1 / 0.075) / 200000000000.0
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 44), 0.25 * strain)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 12), 0.25 * strain)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 25), 0.25 * strain)
        End If

        'This test is sensitive to the effect of transforming BM, BB, BS separately before multiplying with material elasticity.
        'Doing it the normal way (sum BM, BB, BS before transforming) produces more accurate results shown by agreement with
        'Mecway's MITC8, CCX's S4, CCX's S8. However, MSC's CQUAD4 is closer to the "wrong" way of doing MITC4+.
        'The "wrong" way converges to -0.00037 instead of -0.00019
        'Reference value from converged solution and rounded.
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 twisted plate mit4+.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4), -0.00019, 400)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 8), -0.00019, 100)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 12), -0.00019, 20)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 16), -0.00019, 4)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 123), -0.00019, 1)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 412), -0.00019, 1)
        End If

        'SNORM that makes flat plates have a trapezoidal cross section cause membrane-bending coupling
        'due to the offset between the midsurface where loads are applied and the neutral surface at
        'the centroid of the trapezoid.
        'Confirms that the thickness in the direction of the director vector is being calculated correctly.
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 SNORM membrane bending coupling mitc4+.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Reference solution is the equivalent plate with a moment load calculated from the offset of the neutral surface.
            'There are also solid element equivalent meshes which are not used here but show the trapezoidal shape.

            'h = thickness = 0.1
            'F = 1e10
            'd = distance from centroid of trapezoid to midsurface in the +Z direction
            '  = (h/3-h/2) * 0.1/1
            'M_y = F * d
            'M_y = -16666666.66666667
            '
            'h = thickness = 0.001
            'F = 1e7
            'd = distance from centroid of trapezoid to midsurface in the +Z direction
            '  = (h/3-h/2) * 0.001/1
            'M_y = F * d
            'M_y = -16.66666667

            '1x1
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 2), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 50), 0.4)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 3), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 50), 0.4)
            '4x4
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 14), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 54), 0.4)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 26), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 58), 0.4)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 15), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 54), 0.4)

            'Membrane force should behave like a rectangular plate.
            Dim strain As Double = (10000000000.0 / 0.1) / 100000000000.0
            '1x1
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 2), strain, 0.4)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 3), strain, 0.4)
            '4x4
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 14), strain, 0.4)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 26), strain, 0.4)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 15), strain, 0.4)

            'Thin plate
            'Bending
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 63), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 67))
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 64), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 67))
            'Membrane
            strain = (100000000.0 / 0.001) / 100000000000.0
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 63), strain)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 64), strain)

        End If

        '3-digit exponents made using very large element and tiny force.
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 3 digit exponent 1.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Big negative exponent that doesn't fit in single precision is rounded to zero.
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 4), 0) '-1.002287E-105. Theoretically should be zero.
            CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.StressXX, 1, 3), 0) '6.514864E-111. Theoretically should be zero.
        End If

        '3-digit exponents made using a model that happens to generate them.
        'It might fail because of harmless minor changes in Mystran so NAS S30 3 digit exponent 1.liml may test 3-digit exponents instead.
        'The values in the file are negative and they get changed to positive by the (identity) transformation which is fine because
        'they're effectively zero.
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 3 digit exponent 2.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.StressYY, 8, 7), 0) '8.928669E-122
            CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.StressYY, 8, 8), 0) '1.336904E-121
        End If

        Messages.Append("Number of tests")
        CompareDifference(TestCount - initialTestCount, 190)
        functionCount += 1

    End Sub '"

    Private Sub Nastran7Mitc4Plus(testFilesFolder As String, ByRef functionCount As Integer)

        Dim initialTestCount As Integer = TestCount

        Dim solver As SolverType = SolverType.Mystran
        NastranOptions.Mitc4Plus = True

        Dim model As ModelData

        'Displacement, stress for the simplest loading.
        If SolveFile("S30 mitc4 flat membrane normal force.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Stress = 50 MPa
            'Strain  = 50MPa / 200 GPa = 0.00025
            'Displacement = 0.0025 * 0.03 = 7.5e-6
            'Transverse strain = nu * strain = 0.4 * 0.00025 = 0.0001
            'Transverse displacement = 0.021 * 0.0001 = 2.1e-6
            'Reaction force = 50MPa * 1e-3 * 0.021 = 1050 N

            'Displacement
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {2, 3, 6, 7, 10, 11, 14, 15}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, nodeId), 0.0000075)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, nodeId), 0, 1.0E-17)
            Next
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {34, 38, 42, 46}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementMag, nodeId), 0.0000075)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, nodeId), 0, 1.0E-17)
            Next
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {18, 22, 26, 30, 19, 23, 27, 31}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, nodeId), 0.0000075)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, nodeId), 0, 1.0E-17)
            Next
            'Node rotation
            Messages.Append("Node rotation" + vbNewLine)
            Dim max As Double = 0
            For nodeId As Integer = 1 To 48
                'Exclude drilling rotation which is allowed to wander because it's unconstrained.
                If model.Mesh.Z(nodeId) > 0.05 Then
                    max = Math.Max(max, Math.Abs(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, nodeId)))
                End If
                If model.Mesh.X(nodeId) > -0.05 Then
                    max = Math.Max(max, Math.Abs(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationX, nodeId)))
                End If
                max = Math.Max(max, Math.Abs(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, nodeId)))
            Next
            CompareDifference(max, 0, 0.0000000000000001)
            'Transverse displacement
            Messages.Append("Transverse displacement" + vbNewLine)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {3, 7, 11, 15, 31, 27, 23, 19}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, nodeId), -0.0000021)
            Next
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {35, 39, 43, 47}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementMag, nodeId), Math.Sqrt(0.0000021 ^ 2 + 0.0000075 ^ 2))
            Next
            'Stress
            Messages.Append("Stress" + vbNewLine)
            Messages.Append(" U" + vbNewLine)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {1, 2, 9, 11, 34, 35, 41, 44, 30, 32, 21, 22}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressUU), nodeId), 50000000.0)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVV), nodeId), 0, 0.004)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressUV), nodeId), 0, 0.006)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVW), nodeId), 0, 0.000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressWU), nodeId), 0, 0.000001)
            Next
            Messages.Append(" V" + vbNewLine)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {5, 6, 13, 14, 40, 39, 48, 47, 19, 20, 27, 28}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVV), nodeId), 50000000.0)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressUU), nodeId), 0, 0.005)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressUV), nodeId), 0, 0.002)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVW), nodeId), 0, 0.00001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressWU), nodeId), 0, 0.00001)
            Next

        End If

        'Force per length all components only for simple shapes.
        If SolveFile("S30 mitc4 force per length.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            Messages.Append(" U" + vbNewLine)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {1, 2, 3, 4}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, nodeId), 3000000 / 2 / 1.7)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthVW, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthWU, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
            Next
            Messages.Append(" UV" + vbNewLine)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {5, 6, 7, 8}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, nodeId), 3000000 / 2 / 1.7 / 2)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, nodeId), 3000000 / 2 / 1.7 / 2)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, nodeId), -3000000 / 2 / 1.7 / 2)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthVW, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthWU, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
            Next
            Messages.Append(" UW" + vbNewLine)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {9, 10, 11, 12}
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthVW, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthWU, nodeId), 0.003 / 2 / 0.8, 0.002)
            Next
            Messages.Append(" VW" + vbNewLine)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {13, 14, 15, 16}
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthVW, nodeId), -0.003 / 2 / 0.8, 0.001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthWU, nodeId), 0, 0.000000002)
            Next
        End If


        'Reaction force on arbitrary shaped mesh.
        If SolveFile("S30 mitc4 reaction force.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            Dim forceSum As New VectorDouble
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {2, 6, 7, 8}
                forceSum.X += NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionForceX, nodeId)
                forceSum.Y += NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionForceY, nodeId)
                forceSum.Z += NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionForceZ, nodeId)
            Next
            CompareRatioSingle(forceSum.X, -10, 0.0005)
            CompareRatioSingle(forceSum.Y, -20)
            CompareRatioSingle(forceSum.Z, -30, 0.0003)
        End If

        'Reaction moment on rectangular elements with zero prescribed rotation at supports.
        If SolveFile("S30 mitc4 reaction moment easy.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            Dim forceSum As New VectorDouble
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {1, 2, 5, 6}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentX, nodeId), -1.1)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentY, nodeId), 0, 0.0000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentZ, nodeId), 0, 0.0000000001)
            Next
        End If

        'Reaction moment with non-rectangular shape and multiple elements but still flat
        If SolveFile("S30 mitc4 reaction moment nonzero rotation.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {5, 6}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentX, nodeId), -1.1, 0.0001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentY, nodeId), 0, 0.0000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentZ, nodeId), 0, 0.0000000001)
            Next
            CompareTrue(True)
            Dim sum As Double = NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentX, 1) +
                                NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentX, 2) +
                                NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentX, 9)
            CompareRatioSingle(sum, -2.2, 0.0001)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {1, 2, 9}
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentY, nodeId), 0, 0.0000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentZ, nodeId), 0, 0.0000000001)
            Next
        End If

        If SolveFile("S30 mitc4 reaction moment complex shape.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentX, 8), -1.1)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentY, 7), -2.2, 0.0001)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentZ, 6), -3.3)
        End If


        'Strain for simple square shapes
        If SolveFile("S30 mitc4 strain.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'XX
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {9, 11}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), 0.13 / 1.2, 0.0005)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), 0.13 / 1.2, 0.0005)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), 0.13 / 1.2, 0.0005)
            Next
            'XX bending strain 
            'Stress = My/I. M=0.006. y=+/-0.15. I=1.2*0.3^3/12 = 0.333333
            'E=stress/strain. Strain = 0.066666
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {21, 22, 23, 24}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), -0.06666666666666, 0.0001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), 0, 0.00000000001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), 0.06666666666666, 0.0001)
            Next
            'YY
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {13, 14}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), nodeId), 0.13 / 1.2, 0.0005)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), nodeId), 0.13 / 1.2, 0.0005)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), nodeId), 0.13 / 1.2, 0.0005)
            Next
            'XY shear strain
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {1, 2, 3, 4}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXY), nodeId), 0.012 / 1.2, 0.0001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainXY), nodeId), 0.012 / 1.2, 0.0001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainXY), nodeId), 0.012 / 1.2, 0.0001)
            Next
            'YZ shear strain. It's constant through the thickness because of shell idealization.
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {17, 18, 19, 20}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainYZ), nodeId), 0.1 / 1.2, 0.0001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainYZ), nodeId), 0.1 / 1.2, 0.0001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainYZ), nodeId), 0.1 / 1.2, 0.0001)
            Next
            'ZX shear strain. It's constant through the thickness because of shell idealization.
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {5, 6, 7, 8}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainZX), nodeId), 0.1 / 1.2, 0.0001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainZX), nodeId), 0.1 / 1.2, 0.0001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainZX), nodeId), 0.1 / 1.2, 0.0001)
            Next
            'Shear strain rotated with factor of 2 used correctly
            'In element coordinates : gamma_UU = 0, VV=0, UV=0.07/1.2.
            Dim s As Double = Math.Sin(2 * -20 * Math.PI / 180)
            Dim c As Double = Math.Cos(2 * -20 * Math.PI / 180)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {25, 26, 27, 28}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), 0.07 / 1.2 / 2 * s, 0.0001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), nodeId), -0.07 / 1.2 / 2 * s, 0.0001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXY), nodeId), (0.07 / 1.2 / 2 * c) * 2, 0.0001)
            Next

        End If

        ''Both isotropic and laminate materials
        'If SolveFile("S30 mitc4 drilling stiffness.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then

        '    'Direction of rotation matches prescribed rotation
        '    For Each nodeId As Integer In {221, 171, 196, 71, 21, 46}
        '        CompareDifference(Math.Sign(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, nodeId)), 1, 0.1)
        '    Next
        '    For Each nodeId As Integer In {135, 85, 110}
        '        CompareDifference(Math.Sign(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, nodeId)), 1, 0.1)
        '    Next

        '    'Rotation of adjacent nodes is independent of thickness, mesh size, and E and orientation.
        '    Dim oneAngle As Double = model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 210) / model.Mesh.Z(210)
        '    For Each nodeId As Integer In {160, 185}
        '        Dim angle As Double = model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, nodeId) / model.Mesh.Z(nodeId)
        '        CompareRatioSingle(angle, oneAngle)
        '    Next
        '    For Each nodeId As Integer In {60, 10, 35}
        '        Dim angle As Double = model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, nodeId) / -model.Mesh.X(nodeId)
        '        CompareRatioSingle(angle, oneAngle)
        '    Next
        '    For Each nodeId As Integer In {136, 86, 111}
        '        Dim angle As Double = model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, nodeId) / -model.Mesh.Z(nodeId)
        '        CompareRatioSingle(angle, oneAngle)
        '    Next

        '    'No out of plane displacements. Extra tight tolerance required because all displacements are already small.
        '    For Each nodeId As Integer In {160, 185}
        '        CompareDifference(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, nodeId), 0, 1.0E-20)
        '    Next
        '    For Each nodeId As Integer In {60, 10, 35}
        '        CompareDifference(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, nodeId), 0, 1.0E-20)
        '    Next
        '    For Each nodeId As Integer In {136, 86, 111}
        '        CompareDifference(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, nodeId), 0, 1.0E-20)
        '    Next

        '    'Magnitude of rotation is small. This value seems OK but could probably be increased if it's too small.
        '    CompareDifference(Math.Abs(oneAngle), 0.000000000001, 0.000000000001)

        'End If

        If SolveFile("S30 mitc4 macneal membrane patch test.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Reference solution from MacNeal and Harder "A proposed standard set of problems to test finite element accuracy":
            'strain_xx = 1e-3
            'strain_yy = 1e-3
            'strain_xy = 1e-3 (gamma, engineering shear strain)
            'stress_xx = 1333 Pa
            'stress_yy = 1333 Pa
            'stress_xy = 400 Pa

            For nodeId As Integer = 1 To 8
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), 0.001, 0.0005)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), 0.001, 0.0005)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), 0.001, 0.0005)

                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), nodeId), 0.001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), nodeId), 0.001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), nodeId), 0.001)

                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXY), nodeId), 0.001, 0.0001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainXY), nodeId), 0.001, 0.0001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainXY), nodeId), 0.001, 0.0001)

                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressUU), nodeId), 1333, 0.04)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVV), nodeId), 1333, 0.04)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressUV), nodeId), 400, 0.0002)
            Next

            'Stress UU bottom and top
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressUU), 1, 1), 1333, 0.04)
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StressUU), 1, 1), 1333, 0.04)
            'Stress VV bottom and top
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressVV), 1, 1), 1333, 0.04)
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StressVV), 1, 1), 1333, 0.04)
            'Stress UV bottom and top
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressUV), 1, 1), 400, 0.0005)
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StressUV), 1, 1), 400, 0.0005)
        End If


        If SolveFile("S30 mitc4 macneal plate bending patch test.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Reference solution from MacNeal and Harder "A proposed standard set of problems to test finite element accuracy":
            'Bending moment per unit length x = 1.111e-7 N.m/m
            'Bending moment per unit length y = 1.111e-7 N.m/m but that looks like a mistake and it should be 3.33e-8 which
            'matches the analytic integral of stress_xy * z over the thickness.
            'Bending moment per unit length xy = 1e-7 N.m/m
            'stress xx = +/- 0.667 Pa
            'stress yy = +/- 0.667 Pa
            'stress xy = +/- 0.200 Pa

            For nodeId As Integer = 1 To 8
                'Sign convention of moments in Mecway is opposite MacNeal so these are negative.
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthU, nodeId), -0.0000001111, 0.02)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthV, nodeId), -0.0000001111, 0.02)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthUV, nodeId), -0.0000000333, 0.2)
                'Stress UU bottom and top
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressUU), nodeId), 0.667, 0.06)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StressUU), nodeId), -0.667, 0.06)
                'Stress VV bottom and top
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressVV), nodeId), 0.667, 0.06)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StressVV), nodeId), -0.667, 0.06)
                'Stress UV bottom and top
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressUV), nodeId), 0.2, 0.06)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StressUV), nodeId), -0.2, 0.06)
            Next
        End If

        'Simple rectangles
        If SolveFile("S30 mitc4 moment per length.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'U (about V)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {5, 6, 7, 8}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthU, nodeId), 1.1 / 0.8)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthV, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthUV, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
            Next
            'V (about U)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {1, 2, 3, 4}
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthU, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthV, nodeId), 1.1 / 0.8)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthUV, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
            Next
            'UV
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {9, 10, 11, 12}
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthU, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthV, nodeId), 0, 0.000000001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthUV, nodeId), -3.3 * 0.8 / 0.8 / 2)
            Next
            'Combined with rotated axes
            'All 3 should be positive.
            'U And V should be equal by symmetry.
            'All 3 should be equal to half the applied moment because a uniaxial normal stress rotated by 45 degrees becomes x,y,xy stresses each of half the value.
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {13, 14, 15, 16}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthU, nodeId), 1.1 / 0.8 / 2)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthV, nodeId), 1.1 / 0.8 / 2)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthUV, nodeId), 1.1 / 0.8 / 2)
            Next
        End If


        If SolveFile("S30 mitc4 orthotropic membrane.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Strain = stress / E_U = 3/100000
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), 7, 1), 3 / 100000, 0.0001)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), 24), 3 / 100000)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), 13), 3 / 100000, 0.0001)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), 18), 3 / 100000)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), 15), 3 / 100000, 0.0001)

            'Strain = stress / E_V = 3/50000
            CompareRatioSingle(ElementValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), 3, 1), 3 / 50000, 0.0001)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), 7), 3 / 50000, 0.0001)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), 11), 3 / 50000, 0.0001)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), 12), 3 / 50000, 0.0001)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), 8), 3 / 50000, 0.0001)

            'Rotated
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementMag, 2), 3 / 50000 * 1)
            Dim displ3 As New VectorDouble(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 3),
                                           NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 3),
                                           NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 3))
            Dim displ3Longitudinal As Double = displ3.Dot(New VectorDouble(Math.Cos(70 * Math.PI / 180), Math.Sin(70 * Math.PI / 180), 0))
            CompareRatioSingle(displ3Longitudinal, 3 / 50000 * 1)

            'Poisson ratio
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 22), -0.34 * 3 / 100000 * 0.5)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 18), -0.34 * 3 / 100000 * 0.5)
            Dim nu_vu As Double = 0.34 * 50000 / 100000
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 9), -nu_vu * 3 / 50000 * 0.5)

            'Stress
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressUU), 21), 3, 0.0005)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressUU), 17), 3, 0.0005)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVV), 12), 3, 0.0005)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVV), 10), 3, 0.0005)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVV), 6), 3, 0.0005)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVV), 19), 0, 0.0000001)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVV), 24), 0, 0.0000001)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVV), 15), 0, 0.000001)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressUU), 10), 0, 0.0000001)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressUU), 5), 0, 0.0000001)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressUV), 5), 0, 0.000001)
        End If

        If SolveFile("S30 mitc4 orthotropic shear.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Out-of-plane shear stress = 1111 N / 1.54e-5 m^2
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressWU), 8), 1111 / 0.0000154, 0.0001)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVW), 5), 1111 / 0.0000154, 0.0001)
            CompareTrue(True)
            CompareTrue(True)
            'In-plane shear stress
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressUV), 8), 300 / 0.0000077, 0.0002)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressUV), 5), -300 / 0.0000077, 0.0002)
            CompareTrue(True)
            CompareTrue(True)
            'Out-of-plane shear strain. Tests the shear modulii
            Dim shearCorrectionFactor As Double = 5 / 6
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainZX), 8), 1111 / 0.0000154 / 40000000000 / shearCorrectionFactor, 0.0003)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainYZ), 5), 1111 / 0.0000154 / 130000000000 / shearCorrectionFactor)
            CompareTrue(True)
            CompareTrue(True)
            'Out-of-plane shear displacement.
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 8), 1111 / 0.0000154 / 40000000000 * 0.0153333333333333 / shearCorrectionFactor)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 5), 1111 / 0.0000154 / 130000000000 * 0.0153333333333333 / shearCorrectionFactor)
            CompareTrue(True)
            CompareTrue(True)
        End If


        If SolveFile("S30 mitc4 macneal twisted beam.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Reference displacement solutions from MacNeal and Harder "A proposed standard set of problems to test finite element accuracy".

            Project.Configurations.SetActiveConfiguration(Project.Configurations.GetByName("In-plane"))
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 35), 0.005424, 2)
            Dim reactionSum As Double = 0
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {1, 14, 27}
                reactionSum += NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentY, nodeId)
            Next
            CompareRatioSingle(reactionSum, -12, 0.3)

            Project.Configurations.SetActiveConfiguration(Project.Configurations.GetByName("Out-of-plane"))
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 35), 0.001754, 9)
            reactionSum = 0
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {1, 14, 27}
                reactionSum += NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentY, nodeId)
            Next
            CompareDifference(reactionSum, 0, 0.01) 'Must be << 12
        End If


        If SolveFile("S30 mitc4 MacNeal spherical shell.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Reference solutions from MacNeal and Harder "A proposed standard set of problems to test finite element accuracy".
            'Can also be 0.0924 with the center hole filled.
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 12), -0.094, 1)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 1), 0.094, 1)
        End If

        'Shows membrane locking which is overstiffness for thin curved elements. Improved versions of MITC4 should reduce these.
        'MITC4+ is much better and tigher tolerances than the equivalent MITC4 test test that.
        If SolveFile("S30 mitc4 helical mesh ring.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Reference solution taken from meshes with non-skewed elements.
            Dim reference As Double = 0.017
            'Coarse mesh
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 2), reference, 10)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 24), reference, 10)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 18), 0, 0.001)
            'Fine mesh
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 818), reference, 2)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 840), reference, 2)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 834), 0, 0.00001)
        End If

        ''Stress in element coordiantes and displacement.
        ''Layer angle and orientation angle cancel out.
        ''Multiple layers with different thicknesses and angles all have the same physical meaning.
        ''Force per length rotated
        'If SolveFile("S30 mitc4 laminate membrane symmetry.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
        '    CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 7), 0.05)
        '    CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 9), model.Mesh.X(9) / 3 * 0.1)
        '    CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 5), 0.05)
        '    CompareTrue(True)
        '    CompareDifference(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 5), 0, 0.00000000001)
        '    CompareDifference(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 5), 0, 0.00000000001)

        '    'Stress in fiber direction = s = 200e9 * 0.1/3
        '    'Stress rotated 60 degrees = s * cos^2(60)
        '    's * sin^2(60)
        '    's * cos(60) sin(60)
        '    Dim s As Double = Math.Sin(-60 * Math.PI / 180)
        '    Dim c As Double = Math.Cos(-60 * Math.PI / 180)
        '    Dim nodeId As Integer = 4
        '    For layerNo As Integer = 1 To 3
        '        For bottomTop As Integer = -1 To 1 Step 2
        '            CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(LaminateLayerStress(0, layerNo, bottomTop), nodeId), 200000000000.0 * 0.1 / 3 * c ^ 2)
        '            CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(LaminateLayerStress(1, layerNo, bottomTop), nodeId), 200000000000.0 * 0.1 / 3 * s ^ 2)
        '            CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(LaminateLayerStress(2, layerNo, bottomTop), nodeId), 200000000000.0 * 0.1 / 3 * c * s)
        '            nodeId += 1
        '        Next
        '    Next

        '    CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, 9), 200000000000.0 * 0.1 / 3 * c ^ 2 * 0.006)
        '    CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthV, 9), 200000000000.0 * 0.1 / 3 * s ^ 2 * 0.006)
        '    CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthUV, 9), 200000000000.0 * 0.1 / 3 * c * s * 0.006)

        '    CompareDifference(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthVW, 9), 0, 0.0000000001)
        '    CompareDifference(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthWU, 9), 0, 0.0000000001)
        'End If

        ''Moment per length
        ''Reaction moment
        ''Trapezoidal and parallelogram elements
        'If SolveFile("S30 mitc4 laminate moment per length.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
        '    Dim moment As Double = -80000 / 0.25
        '    CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthV, 1), moment, 0.3)
        '    CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthV, 2), moment, 0.1)
        '    CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthV, 8), moment, 0.05)
        '    CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthV, 5), moment, 0.05)
        '    CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.MomentPerLengthV, 4), moment, 0.05)

        '    CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentX, 1) +
        '                 model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentX, 7), -80000)
        'End If


        ''Out-of-plane shear stiffness
        ''Out-of-plane shear force per length
        'If SolveFile("S30 mitc4 laminate shear.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
        '    For nodeId As Integer = 1 To 4
        '        CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthVW, nodeId), -1.23 / 2 / 1.4)
        '    Next
        '    'test the correct stiffness component was used. Reference is an orthotropic material.
        '    'Error is due to shear correction factor difference betwene laminate and orthotropic.
        '    CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 4),
        '                 model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 8), 15)
        'End If

        ''General 3D shape
        'If SolveFile("S30 mitc4 laminate arbitrary shape.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
        '    'Reference solution from refined solid mesh
        '    CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 1886), -0.00000179110475105299, 30)
        '    CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 1886), 0.0000100579348176529, 20)
        '    CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 1886), -0.00000210785330005945, 2)
        '    'Reference solution from solid mesh in the same file
        '    CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableNames.LaminateLayerVariable(FieldVariableType.StressVonMises, 2, 1), 1879), 38023.2040385993, 70)
        '    CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(FieldVariableNames.Bottom(FieldVariableType.StressVonMises), 1879), 60691.8926301899, 30)
        '    CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressUU), 1900), -29328, 20)
        '    CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressVV), 1900), -3609, 20)
        '    CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StressUV), 1900), 7070, 30)
        '    CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(LaminateLayerStress(0, 2, 1), 1900), 23911, 10)
        '    CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(LaminateLayerStress(1, 2, 1), 1900), 9410, 20)
        '    CompareRatioSingle(model.FieldVariables.NodeValue(LaminateLayerStress(2, 2, 1), 1900), -4655, 40)
        'End If

        'Unconstrained expansion with uniform temperature (stress-free).
        'Displacement
        'Strain
        'Non-rectangular elements
        'Orthotropic
        'Zero stress
        'von Mises with Nastran shells
        If SolveFile("S30 mitc4 thermal stress free expansion.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            Dim strainU As Double = (310 - 300) * 0.003
            Dim strainV As Double = (310 - 300) * 0.005
            'Displacement
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {3, 7, 12, 13, 14, 16, 17, 18, 19, 20}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, nodeId), model.Mesh.X(nodeId) * strainU, 0.0001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, nodeId), model.Mesh.Y(nodeId) * strainV)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, nodeId), 0, 0.00000000001)
            Next
            'Strain
            Messages.Append("Strain" & vbNewLine)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {3, 4, 5, 12, 13, 17}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), strainU, 0.0005)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), strainU, 0.0005)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), nodeId), strainV, 0.0005)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), nodeId), strainV, 0.0005)
            Next
            'Angled
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {23, 27}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementMag, nodeId), Math.Sqrt((2 * strainU) ^ 2 + (2 * strainV) ^ 2), 0.0001)
            Next
            'Stress
            Messages.Append("Stress" & vbNewLine)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {4, 9, 16, 23, 26}
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableNames.Bottom(FieldVariableType.StressVonMises), nodeId), 0, 0.005)
            Next
        End If


        'Isotropic. This is an easy-mode version of S30 mitc4 thermal strain free diamond cylinder.liml with smaller error.
        MitcCylinderAxis = New VectorDouble(0, 1, 0)
        If SolveFile("S30 mitc4 thermal strain free rectangle cylinder.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Reference solution from orthotropic solid with 0 thermal expansion coefficient in the thickness direction.
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {56, 66}
                'Must be << 0.18 m
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, nodeId), 0, 0.000000000001)
            Next
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {164, 165}
                'Must be << 0.18 m
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, nodeId), 0, 0.00000001)
            Next
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {50, 59}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, nodeId), 0.141, 0.1)
            Next
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {78, 158}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, nodeId), 0.141, 0.5)
            Next
            'Reference solution from thermal strain hand calc
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {45, 56, 141, 76}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, nodeId), 3 * 0.0003 * 100)
            Next
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainZX), 59), 0, 0.0000000000001)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainZX), 157), 0, 0.0000000000001)

            'Stress/strain/force are not very good, probably because they're calculated without considering SNORM.
            If True Then
                'Compare to existing solution to detect any improvement.

                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), 50), 0.0283771)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), 158), 0.0298996)
                CompareDifference(ElementValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StressVonMises), 25, 1), 2629282437.2192, 1000)
                CompareDifference(ElementValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVonMises), 57, 3), 641892787.524755, 100)
                CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthWU, 24, 2), 0, 0.00001)
                CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthVW, 24, 2), 150694500, 10)
            Else
                'Proper tests for if Mystran is improved.

                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), 50), 0.0003 * 100)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), 158), 0.0003 * 100)
                CompareDifference(ElementValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StressVonMises), 25, 1), 0, 0.0006)
                CompareDifference(ElementValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressVonMises), 57, 3), 0, 0.0005)
                CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthWU, 24, 2), 0, 0.000001)
                CompareDifference(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthVW, 24, 2), 0, 0.000001)
            End If
        End If
        MitcCylinderAxis = Nothing

        'Nonplanar elements
        'Orthotropic
        MitcCylinderAxis = New VectorDouble(0, 1, 0)
        If SolveFile("S30 mitc4 thermal strain free diamond cylinder.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Reference solution from orthotropic solid with 0 thermal expansion coefficient in the thickness direction.
            'u_x must be << 0.18 m
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {243, 259} 'Coarse mesh
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, nodeId), 0, 0.01)
            Next
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {58, 66} 'Fine mesh
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, nodeId), 0, 0.001)
            Next
            'Coarse mesh
            CompareRatioSingle((NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 266) +
                          NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 267)) / 2, 0.0937, 1)
            CompareRatioSingle((NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 239) +
                          NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 242)) / 2, 0.0937, 2)
            'Fine mesh
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {63, 200}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, nodeId), 0.0937, 0.8)
            Next

            For Each nodeId As Integer In {249, 271} 'Coarse mesh
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, nodeId), 3 * 0.0007 * 100, 1)
            Next
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {23, 45} 'Fine mesh
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, nodeId), 3 * 0.0007 * 100, 0.1)
            Next
