        CompareDifference(TestCount - initialTestCount, 387)

        functionCount += 1

    End Sub '"

    Private Sub Nastran10(testFilesFolder As String, ByRef functionCount As Integer)
        Dim initialTestCount As Integer = TestCount

        Dim model As ModelData

        'Make sure 0 is used because 0 values are omitted from the F06 file.
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 all zero SPC forces.liml", testFilesFolder, model, SolverType.Mystran) Then
            'These must be zero becuase they're constraints.
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionForceMag, 4), 0)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentX, 4), 0)
            'These would be OK as novalue or 0 because they have no constraints.
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionForceZ, 3), 0)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentZ, 3), 0)
        End If

        'Make sure 0 is used because 0 values are omitted from the F06 file.
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 all zero MPC forces.liml", testFilesFolder, model, SolverType.Mystran) Then
            'These would be OK as novalue or 0 because they have no constraints.
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionForceMag, 4), 0)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentX, 4), 0)
            'These must be zero becuase they're constraints.
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionForceZ, 3), 0)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentZ, 3), 0)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionForceY, 11), 0)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentY, 11), 0)
        End If

        'Make sure AUTOSPC forces are included in reaction forces.
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 AUTOSPC reaction forces.liml", testFilesFolder, model, SolverType.Mystran) Then
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionForceX, 10), -11111)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionMomentZ, 9), -22222)
        End If

        'Regression test for Mystran using wrong Gauss points to extrapolate stress to corner nodes.
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 MITC4+ corner stress order.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Reference solution from internal solver.
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {2, 3, 6, 7, 10, 11, 14, 15}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressUU), nodeId), -75)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), -0.0000025)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, nodeId), -1.5)
            Next
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {1, 4, 5, 8, 9, 12, 13, 16}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StressUU), nodeId), 25)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), 0.000000833333333333333, 0.0001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ForcePerLengthU, nodeId), 0.5)
            Next
        End If

        'Comments for Nastran export.
        'Main test of word wrapping algorithm.
        If OpenFile("NAS comment.liml", testFilesFolder) IsNot Nothing Then
            Dim outputFileName As String = testFilesFolder & "/NAS comment OUTPUT.bdf"
            IO.File.Delete(outputFileName)
            Dim writer As New NastranWriter(outputFileName, Project.ModelerModel, Project.SolutionObjects, UIState.MystranAutoFieldFormat)
            writer.WriteFile()
            Dim fileContents As String() = IO.File.ReadAllLines(outputFileName)

            'Each of the 3 comments could be in a different order if I change the logic for how they're ordered.
            'Don't use FindLine because it trims them and we're checking for trailing spaces in some cases.

            'Normal paragraph with word wrappings.
            CompareString(fileContents(1), "$ But I must explain to you how all this mistaken idea of denouncing pleasure")
            CompareString(fileContents(2), "$ and praising pain was born and I will give you a complete account of the")
            CompareString(fileContents(3), "$ system, and expound the actual teachings of the great explorer of the truth,")
            CompareString(fileContents(4), "$ the master-builder of human happiness. No one rejects, dislikes, or avoids")
            CompareString(fileContents(5), "$ pleasure itself, because it is pleasure, but because those who do not know how")
            CompareString(fileContents(6), "$ to pursue pleasure rationally encounter consequences that are extremely")
            'Short line because it's followed by a huge word.
            CompareString(fileContents(7), "$ painful.")
            'Splits a word that's longer than a line can be.
            CompareString(fileContents(8), "$ VeryLongWordThat'sMoreThanTwoLines1.VeryLongWordThat'sMoreThanTwoLines2.VeryLo")
            CompareString(fileContents(9), "$ ngWordThat'sMoreThanTwoLines3.VeryLongWordThat'sMoreThanTwoLines.VeryLongWordT")
            CompareString(fileContents(10), "$ hat'sMoreThanTwoLines4.VeryLongWordThat'sMoreThanTwoLines5.VeryLongWordThat'sM")
            CompareString(fileContents(11), "$ oreThanTwoLines6. Nor again is there anyone who loves or pursues or desires to")
            'Continue normal paragraph word wrapping.
            CompareString(fileContents(12), "$ obtain pain of itself, because it is pain, but because occasionally")
            CompareString(fileContents(13), "$ circumstances occur in which toil and pain can procure him some great")
            CompareString(fileContents(14), "$ pleasure. To take a trivial example, which of us ever undertakes laborious")
            CompareString(fileContents(15), "$ physical exercise, except to obtain some advantage from it? But who has any")
            CompareString(fileContents(16), "$ right to find fault with a man who chooses to enjoy a pleasure that has no")
            CompareString(fileContents(17), "$ annoying consequences, or one who avoids a pain that produces no resultant")
            CompareString(fileContents(18), "$ pleasure?")
            'Explicit line breaks
            CompareString(fileContents(19), "$ Line2")
            CompareString(fileContents(20), "$ Line3")

            'Comment beginning with blank line and containing trailing spaces on some lines.
            CompareString(fileContents(21), "$   ")
            CompareString(fileContents(22), "$ Line 2")
            CompareString(fileContents(23), "$ Line 3   ")
            CompareString(fileContents(24), "$ Line 4")
            CompareString(fileContents(25), "$ ")
            CompareString(fileContents(26), "$ ")

            CompareString(fileContents(27), "$ The next line should preserve the number of spaces between words.")
            CompareString(fileContents(28), "$ 2spaces  3spaces   4spaces    endofline")
            CompareString(fileContents(29), "$ ")
            CompareString(fileContents(30), "$ The next line should wrap anywhere between the final 4 and ""after"".")
            CompareString(fileContents(31), "$ 12345678901234567890123456789012345678901234567890123456789012345678901234    ")
            CompareString(fileContents(32), "$    after 8 spaces.")
            CompareString(fileContents(33), "$ ")
            CompareString(fileContents(34), "$ The next 3 lines are have exactly the maximum line length. Should not insert")
            CompareString(fileContents(35), "$ extra newlines after any of them.")
            CompareString(fileContents(36), "$ 123456789012345678901234567890123456789012345678901234567890123456789012345678")
            CompareString(fileContents(37), "$ 123456789012345678901234567890123456789012345678901234567890123456789012345678")
            CompareString(fileContents(38), "$ 123456789012345678901234567890123456789012345678901234567890123456789012345678")

            'First line of executive control section with nothing else before it.
            CompareString(fileContents(39), "$-----------------------EXECUTIVE CONTROL SECTION-----------------------")

        End If


        If OpenFile("NAS comment configurations.liml", testFilesFolder) IsNot Nothing Then
            Dim outputFileName As String = testFilesFolder & "/NAS comment configurations OUTPUT.bdf"

            Dim writer As NastranWriter
            Dim fileContents As String()

            IO.File.Delete(outputFileName)
            Project.Configurations.SetActiveConfiguration(Project.Configurations.GetByName("Configuration"))
            writer = New NastranWriter(outputFileName, Project.ModelerModel, Project.SolutionObjects, UIState.MystranAutoFieldFormat)
            writer.WriteFile()
            fileContents = IO.File.ReadAllLines(outputFileName)
            CompareString(fileContents(1), "$ conf1")
            CompareString(fileContents(2), "$-----------------------EXECUTIVE CONTROL SECTION-----------------------")

            IO.File.Delete(outputFileName)
            Project.Configurations.SetActiveConfiguration(Project.Configurations.GetByName("Configuration(2)"))
            writer = New NastranWriter(outputFileName, Project.ModelerModel, Project.SolutionObjects, UIState.MystranAutoFieldFormat)
            writer.WriteFile()
            fileContents = IO.File.ReadAllLines(outputFileName)
            CompareString(fileContents(1), "$ conf2")
            CompareString(fileContents(2), "$-----------------------EXECUTIVE CONTROL SECTION-----------------------")

        End If

        'MITC4+ Raasch hook works correctly with SNORM set.
        'SNORM values converted from node coordinates with a spreadsheet and translation by the two curves' centers.
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 mitc4 Raasch hook SNORM.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 40), 5 * 0.0254, 15)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 98), 5 * 0.0254, 6)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 311), 5 * 0.0254, 2)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 2648), 5 * 0.0254, 0.1)
        End If

        If SolveFile("NAS S30 mitc8 Raasch hook.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 40), 5 * 0.0254, 4)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 98), 5 * 0.0254, 5)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 311), 5 * 0.0254, 15)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 2648), 5 * 0.0254, 3)
        End If


        'QUAD4, TRIA3, QUADD8 (not there)
        'Orthotropic, composite
        'Axis-aligned, rotated
        'Different scales
        'Reference values from MSC truncated to the clean digits to exclude the effect of membrane stiffness.
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 K6ROT.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Axis aligned elements
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 3), 0.00011111, 0.01) 'CQUAD4 orthotropic
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 18), 0) 'CQUAD8
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 7), 0.00005555, 0.1) 'CTRIA3
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 43), 0.00011111, 0.01) 'CQUAD4 composite
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 11), 0.00011111, 0.01) 'CQUAD4 scaled
            'Rotated elements
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 23), 0.00011111 / Math.Sqrt(2), 0.01) 'CQUAD4 orthotropic
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 27), 0.00005555 / Math.Sqrt(2), 0.1) 'CTRIA3
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 47), 0.00011111 / Math.Sqrt(2), 0.01) 'CQUAD4 composite
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 31), 0.00011111 / Math.Sqrt(2), 0.01) 'CQUAD4 scaled
        End If

        'QUAD4 (MITC4+), TRIA3, QUADD8 (not there)
        'Orthotropic, isotropic
        'Axis-aligned, rotated
        'Different scales
        'Reference values from MSC truncated to the clean digits to exclude the effect of membrane stiffness.
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 K6ROT MITC4+.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            'Axis aligned elements
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 3), 0.00011111, 0.01) 'CQUAD4 orthotropic
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 18), 0) 'CQUAD8
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 7), 0.00005555, 0.1) 'CTRIA3
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 43), 0.00011111, 0.01) 'CQUAD4 isotropic
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 11), 0.00011111, 0.01) 'CQUAD4 scaled
            'Rotated elements
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 23), 0.00011111 / Math.Sqrt(2), 0.01) 'CQUAD4 orthotropic
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 27), 0.00005555 / Math.Sqrt(2), 0.1) 'CTRIA3
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 47), 0.00011111 / Math.Sqrt(2), 0.01) 'CQUAD4 isotropic
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 31), 0.00011111 / Math.Sqrt(2), 0.01) 'CQUAD4 scaled
        End If


        'Strain for simple square shapes
        If SolveFile("S30 mitc3 strain.liml", testFilesFolder, model, SolverType.Mystran) Then
            Messages.Append(" XX" + vbNewLine)
            'XX
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {9, 11}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), 0.13 / 1.2, 0.0005)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), 0.13 / 1.2, 0.0005)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), 0.13 / 1.2, 0.0005)
            Next
            Messages.Append(" XX bending" + vbNewLine)
            'XX bending strain 
            'Stress = My/I. M=0.006. y=+/-0.15. I=1.2*0.3^3/12 = 0.333333
            'E=stress/strain. Strain = 0.066666
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {21, 22, 23, 24}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), -0.06666666666666, 0.0001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), 0, 0.00000000001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), 0.06666666666666, 0.0001)
            Next
            'YY
            Messages.Append(" YY" + vbNewLine)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {13, 14}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), nodeId), 0.13 / 1.2, 0.0005)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), nodeId), 0.13 / 1.2, 0.0005)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), nodeId), 0.13 / 1.2, 0.0005)
            Next
            'XY shear strain
            Messages.Append(" XY" + vbNewLine)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {1, 2, 3, 4}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXY), nodeId), 0.012 / 1.2)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainXY), nodeId), 0.012 / 1.2)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainXY), nodeId), 0.012 / 1.2)
            Next
            Messages.Append("Transverse shear" & vbNewLine)
            'YZ shear strain. It's constant through the thickness because of shell idealization.
            Messages.Append(" YZ" + vbNewLine)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {17, 18, 19, 20}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainYZ), nodeId), 0.1 / 1.2, 0.0001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainYZ), nodeId), 0.1 / 1.2, 0.0001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainYZ), nodeId), 0.1 / 1.2, 0.0001)
            Next
            'ZX shear strain. It's constant through the thickness because of shell idealization.
            Messages.Append(" ZX" + vbNewLine)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {5, 6, 7, 8}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainZX), nodeId), 0.1 / 1.2, 0.0001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Mid(FieldVariableType.StrainZX), nodeId), 0.1 / 1.2, 0.0001)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Top(FieldVariableType.StrainZX), nodeId), 0.1 / 1.2, 0.0001)
            Next
            'Shear strain rotated with factor of 2 used correctly
            'In element coordinates : gamma_UU = 0, VV=0, UV=0.07/1.2.
            Messages.Append(" XX,YY,XY rotated" + vbNewLine)
            Dim s As Double = Math.Sin(2 * -20 * Math.PI / 180)
            Dim c As Double = Math.Cos(2 * -20 * Math.PI / 180)
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {25, 26, 27, 28}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXX), nodeId), 0.07 / 1.2 / 2 * s, 0.0002)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainYY), nodeId), -0.07 / 1.2 / 2 * s, 0.0002)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(Layer1Bot(FieldVariableType.StrainXY), nodeId), (0.07 / 1.2 / 2 * c) * 2, 0.0002)
            Next

        End If


        'Tests fix of issue: https://github.com/MystranSolver/MYSTRANSolver/issues/180
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 tiny principal stress and strain angle bug.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            If Comlab Then

                For i As Integer = 1 To 6
                    CompareTrue(False)
                Next

            Else

                Dim f06Name As String
                If ReadWriteRead Then
                    f06Name = "testsave.f06"
                Else
                    f06Name = "NAS_S30_tiny_principal_stress_and_strain_angle_bug.f06"
                End If
                Dim f06contents As String() = IO.File.ReadAllLines(TempFolder & "mystran" & "/" & f06Name)
                Dim position As Integer = 0

                FindLine("E L E M E N T   S T R E S S E S   I N   L O C A L   E L E M E N T   C O O R D I N A T E   S Y S T E M", f06contents, True, position)
                Dim line As String = FindLine("1  CENTER", f06contents, True, position)
                Dim angle As Double = ValDbl(line.Substring(74 - 1, 7))
                'abs because both +90 and -90 are the same principal direciton and both correct.
                CompareRatioSingle(Math.Abs(angle), 90)

                FindLine("E L E M E N T   S T R A I N S   I N   L O C A L   E L E M E N T   C O O R D I N A T E   S Y S T E M", f06contents, True, position)
                line = FindLine("1  CENTER", f06contents, True, position)
                angle = ValDbl(line.Substring(74 - 1, 7))
                'abs because both +90 and -90 are the same principal direction and both correct.
                CompareRatioSingle(Math.Abs(angle), 90)

            End If

        End If


        Messages.Append("Number of tests")
        CompareDifference(TestCount - initialTestCount, 219)
        functionCount += 1

    End Sub '"

    Private Sub Nastran11DofReleaseFrequency(testFilesFolder As String, ByRef functionCount As Integer)
        Dim initialTestCount As Integer = TestCount

        Dim model As ModelData

        'Beam dof release for frequency. These tests are important because DOF release on the mass matrix is different to and more complicated than on the stiffness matrix.

        'Beam dof release (flexible joint on beam) has 9 special cases with the internal solver so each one needs a test.
        'Others are combinations of these independent cases, such as x-y (x-n and n-y) or yz-n (y-n and z-n).

        '              node 2
        '          | n rx ry rz 
        '       ---+-----------
        '       n  |   y  y  y
        'node 1 rx | y y
        '       ry | y    y
        '       rz | y       y

        'Test cases are:
        'node 1 rx,   node 2 none
        'node 1 ry,   node 2 none
        'node 1 rz,   node 2 none
        'node 1 none, node 2 rx
        'node 1 none, node 2 ry
        'node 1 none, node 2 rz
        'node 1 rx,   node 2 rx
        'node 1 ry,   node 2 ry
        'node 1 rz,   node 2 rz
        'Rotational invariance


        'In the files, solver is set to OpenRadioss to make sure it won't solve without explicitly setting it here.
        Dim solver As SolverType = SolverType.Mystran

        For Each fileName As String In {"V30 beam released dof x-n.liml", "V30 beam released dof n-x.liml"}
            If SolveFile(fileName, testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
                'Make sure the element is oriented as expected.
                CompareDifference(model.Element(1).Link(1), If(fileName.Contains("x-n"), 3, 2))
                CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(7), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(8))
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4, 7) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4, 8),
                                  -NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 5, 7) + -NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 5, 8))
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 3, 7) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 3, 8), 0, 0.000000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 3, 7) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 3, 8), 0, 0.000000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 3, 7) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 3, 8), 0, 0.000000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationX, 3, 7) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationX, 3, 8), 0, 0.000000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, 3, 7) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, 3, 8), 0, 0.000000000001)
                CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 3, 7) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 3, 8), 0, 0.000000000001)


            End If
        Next

        If SolveFile("V30 beam released dof x-n slender.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(1), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(2))
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(7), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(8)) 'Pin mode
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(9), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(10))
        End If


        For Each fileName As String In {"V30 beam released dof y-n.liml", "V30 beam released dof n-y.liml"}
            If SolveFile(fileName, testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
                'Make sure the element is oriented as expected.
                CompareDifference(model.Element(1).Link(1), If(fileName.Contains("y-n"), 3, 2))
                'All modes should be the same between the two equivalent structures.
                CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(1), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(2))
                CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(3), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(4))
                CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(5), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(6))
                CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(7), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(8))
                CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(9), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(10))

                'Z-bending. The most important one.
                'Reference solution from the equivalent structure in the same file.
                CompareRatioSingle((NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 3, 1) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 3, 2)) _
                                 / (NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4, 1) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4, 2)),
                             0.2032675 / 0.09932444, 0.00006)

                'Y-bending
                'Reference solution from the equivalent structure in the same file.
                CompareRatioSingle((NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 3, 5) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 3, 6)) _
                                 / (NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 4, 5) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 4, 6)),
                               -0.1383151 / -0.146867, 0.0004)

                'X-torsion
                'Reference solution from the equivalent structure in the same file.
                CompareRatioSingle((NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4, 9) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4, 10)) _
                                 / (NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 5, 9) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 5, 10)),
                               -1)

            End If
        Next

        For Each fileName As String In {"V30 beam released dof z-n.liml", "V30 beam released dof n-z.liml"}
            If SolveFile(fileName, testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
                'Make sure the element is oriented as expected.
                CompareDifference(model.Element(1).Link(1), If(fileName.Contains("z-n"), 3, 2))
                'All modes should be the same between the two equivalent structures.
                CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(1), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(2))
                CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(3), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(4))
                CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(5), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(6))
                CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(7), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(8))
                CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(9), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(10))

                'Y-bending
                'Reference solution from the equivalent structure in the same file.
                CompareRatioSingle((NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 3, 3) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 3, 4)) _
                                 / (NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 4, 3) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 4, 4)),
                        0.2029788 / 0.09907166, 0.0001)

            End If

        Next


        If SolveFile("V30 beam released dof x-x.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Axial rotation released at both ends is not valid in Mystran.
            'Make sure there's not solution, not a wrong solution.
            CompareDifference(model.Modes.Count, 0)
            CompareTrue(Not model.FieldVariables.HasVariable(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX))
            For i As Integer = 1 To 8
                CompareTrue(True)
            Next
        End If

        If SolveFile("V30 beam released dof y-y.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'All modes should be the same between the two equivalent structures.
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(1), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(2))
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(3), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(4))
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(5), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(6))
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(7), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(8))
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(9), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(10))

            'Z-bending at node 2.
            'Reference solution from the equivalent structure in the same file.
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 3, 1) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 3, 2), 0, 0.0000000000001)
            CompareRatioSingle((NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 5, 1) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 5, 2)) _
                             / (NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4, 1) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4, 2)),
                               1)
            CompareRatioSingle((NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, 2, 1) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, 2, 2)) _
                             / (NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 2, 1) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 2, 2)),
                                0.5844337 / 0.1948112, 0.00005)

            'Z-bending at node 1.
            'Reference solution from the equivalent structure in the same file.
            CompareRatioSingle((NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, 3, 3) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, 3, 4)) /
                               (NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 3, 3) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 3, 4)),
                               0.6933178 / -0.2311059, 0.00005)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, 2, 3) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, 2, 4), 0, 0.000000000001)


            'Y-bending
            'Reference solution from the equivalent structure in the same file.
            CompareRatioSingle((NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 3, 5) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 3, 6)) _
                             / (NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 4, 5) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 4, 6)),
                          -0.1247509 / -0.1324642, 0.0002)

            'X-torsion
            'Reference solution from the equivalent structure in the same file.
            CompareRatioSingle((NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4, 9) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4, 10)) _
                             / (NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 5, 9) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 5, 10)),
                               -1)

        End If

        If SolveFile("V30 beam released dof z-z.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'All modes should be the same between the two equivalent structures.
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(1), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(2))
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(3), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(4))
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(5), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(6))
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(7), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(8))
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(9), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(10))

            'Y-bending at node 2.
            'Reference solution from the equivalent structure in the same file.
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 3, 3) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 3, 4), 0, 0.0000000000001)
            CompareRatioSingle((NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 5, 3) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 5, 4)) _
                             / (NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 4, 3) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 4, 4)),
                               1)
            CompareRatioSingle((NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 2, 3) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 2, 4)) _
                             / (NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 2, 3) + NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 2, 4)),
                               -0.5864209 / 0.1945833, 0.00005)

        End If


        If SolveFile("V30 beam released dof y-z rotated.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'All modes should be the same between the three equivalent structures.
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(2), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(1))
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(3), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(1))
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(5), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(4))
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(6), model.Modes.NativeFrequency(4))
        End If

        If SolveFile("V30 beam released dof xyz-xyz.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Make sure there's not solution, not a wrong solution.
            CompareDifference(model.Modes.Count, 0)
            CompareTrue(Not model.FieldVariables.HasVariable(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX))
            For i As Integer = 1 To 5
                CompareTrue(True)
            Next
        End If

        Messages.Append("Number of tests")
        CompareDifference(TestCount - initialTestCount, 106)
        functionCount += 1

    End Sub '"

    Private Sub Nastran12(testFilesFolder As String, ByRef functionCount As Integer)
        Dim initialTestCount As Integer = TestCount

        Dim model As ModelData

        Dim solver As SolverType = SolverType.Mystran

        'Multiple custom bulk data's written in the correct order.
        'Suppressed one not written.
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 multiple custom texts.liml", testFilesFolder, model) Then
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 8), 0.005)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 3), 0.005)
        End If

        'Also tests MPCs with 4+ terms
        If SolveFile("S30 Shell pinned support multiple nodes.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 1), -0.1)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 2), -0.1)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 3), 0.1)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 4), 0.1)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 1), 0.1)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 2), -0.1)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 3), -0.1)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 4), 0.1)
        End If

        'Also tests MPCs with 4+ terms
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 Solid pinned support nonuniform face sizes.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Ensure there's some nonzero solution
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 20), -0.1875)
            'Symmetric deformation.
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 7), -NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 8))
            'Axis of rotation is at the center of the surface
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 11), 0, 0.0000000000001)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 12), 0, 0.0000000000001)
        End If

        If SolveFile("NAS V30 hex20 pinned support.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Reference values from internal solver
            'Exact match because probably identical hex20 formulations.
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(1), 287.6472)
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(2), 287.6472)
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(3), 792.3835)
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(4), 1084.762)
            'Axial twisting mode
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 23, 3), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 23, 3))
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 28, 3), -NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 28, 3))
            'S-shaped mode
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementMag, 6, 4), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementMag, 30, 4), 0.009)
        End If


        'Multiple MPCs with 2 terms each, behaving the same as coupled DOF.
        If SolveFile("S30 Solid MPC 2 terms coupled.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Same displacement on each node of each MPC pair
            CompareRatio(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 5), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 5 + 4), 0.00000000001)
            CompareRatio(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 5), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 5 + 4), 0.00000000001)
            CompareRatio(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 5), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 5 + 4), 0.00000000001)
            CompareRatio(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementMag, 6), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementMag, 6 + 4), 0.00000000001)
            CompareRatio(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementMag, 7), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementMag, 7 + 4), 0.00000000001)
            CompareRatio(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementMag, 8), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementMag, 8 + 4), 0.00000000001)
            'Compare stress to the model without MPCs
            CompareRatio(ElementValue(FieldVariableType.StressVonMises, 1, 7), ElementValue(FieldVariableType.StressVonMises, 3, 7), 0.00000000001)
            'Make sure SPCs are still enforced.
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 2), 0, 0.00000000001)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 2), 0, 0.00000000001)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 3), 0, 0.00000000001)
        End If


        'Rotational DOFs.
        'Also a non-zero displacement constraint on a retained DOF. It's also the same node as some condensed rotational DOFs but they must not interfere.
        If SolveFile("S30 Beam MPC on constrained dof.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Prescribed displacement on a retained DOF and appearing on the condensed dof
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 2), 0.00001)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 3), 0.00001)
            'Check that it behaves the same as a single combined beam
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 4), -0.00007247967)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 10), 0.00001422365)
            'Half the rotation at half the length
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationX, 2), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationX, 4) / 2)
            'Half the stretching at half the length
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 2), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 4) / 2, 0.00005)
            'Same rotation on both coupled nodes
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationX, 3), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationX, 4) / 2)
        End If


        'Simple nonhomogenous MPC Ux4 - Ux2 = 0.2m
        If SolveFile("S30 Beam MPC nonhomogenous.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Long beam EA/l = 80N/m
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 2), 4 / 80)
            'Short beam EA/l = 160N/m
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 3) - NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 4), 4 / 160, 0.00005)
            'Enforcement of equation
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 2), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 4) - 0.2)
            'Reaction forces
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionForceX, 1), -4)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionForceX, 2), 4)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.ReactionForceX, 4), -4)
            'No out of plane displacement
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 2), 0, 0.00000000001)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4), 0, 0.00000000001)
            'Make sure SPCs are still enforced.
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 1), 0, 0.00000000001)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 1), 0, 0.00000000001)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationX, 1), 0, 0.00000000001)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationY, 3), 0, 0.00000000001)
        End If


        'Constraint equation in frequency. All 6 DOFs
        If SolveFile("V30 Beam MPC on constrained dof.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Mode 1 should be 0 Hz
            CompareDifference(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(1), 0, 0.00001)
            'Frequencies taken from complete beam without constraint equations
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(2), 58.76829)
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(3), 99.87421)
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(4), 303.0141)
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(5), 318.5847)
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(6), 486.1439)
            'Mode 1 is rigid body rotation. Same slope along the beam.
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 4, 1) - NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 8, 1),
                                   NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 10, 1) - NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 5, 1), 0.00005)
            'Mode 4 displacement ratio compared to without constraint equations
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 5, 4) / NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 10, 4), 0.26789 / 0.1732128)
            'No torsion mode because Mystran beams don't have axial rotational intertia.
        End If

        'Up to 3 constriant equations sharing the same master DOF in the eigenvalue solver
        'The 2 small squares have lower density to ensure that all of the modes are rigid body modes for them - all their deformation modes are too high in frequency.
        If SolveFile("V20 Constraint Equations shared masters.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            '3 rigid body modes - combinations of translation in X, translation of big block in Y, and rotation of big block.
            CompareDifference(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(1), 0, 0.0001)
            CompareDifference(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(2), 0, 0.0001)
            CompareDifference(model.Modes.NativeFrequency(3), 0, 0.0001)
            Dim averageUx As Double
            For modeNo As Integer = 1 To 9 Step 2 'Only check some modes to avoid bloating the test results
                'Make sure the CEs are enforced
                averageUx = (NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 24, modeNo) +
                                 NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 4, modeNo) +
                                 NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 25, modeNo) +
                                 NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 27, modeNo)) / 4
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 28, modeNo), averageUx, 0.0005)
                averageUx = (NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 25, modeNo) +
                                 NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 27, modeNo) +
                                 NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 21, modeNo) +
                                 NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 17, modeNo)) / 4
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 30, modeNo), averageUx, 0.0005)
                averageUx = (NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 27, modeNo) +
                                 NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 21, modeNo) +
                                 NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 17, modeNo) +
                                 NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 22, modeNo)) / 4
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 8, modeNo), averageUx, 0.002)
                'Top square is doing a rigid body rotation. That is, the displacement enforced by the CE is actually recognized by the rest of the mesh.
                CompareRatio(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 11, modeNo) / NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 28, modeNo), 1, 0.001)
                CompareRatio(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 29, modeNo) / NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 30, modeNo), 1, 0.003)
            Next
        End If

        '9 nonhomogenous MPCs to test large number of SPOINTs.
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 Beam MPC nonhomogenous 9 equations.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            For Each nodeId As Integer In {3, 7, 11, 15, 19, 23, 27, 31, 35}
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, nodeId), 0.03 + 0.01)
                CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, nodeId), (0.03 + 0.01) * 0.5)
            Next
        End If

        'Nodes that isn't part of any element but is used by constraint equations as master.
        If SolveFile("B21 Constraint equations isolated node.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            CompareRatio(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 9, 2), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 18, 2), 0.00000000001)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 9, 2), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 18, 2), 0)
            CompareRatio(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 9, 2), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 18, 2), 0.00000000001)
            CompareRatio(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 9, 2), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 19, 2), 0.00000000001)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 9, 2), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 19, 2))
            CompareRatio(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 9, 2), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 19, 2), 0.00000000001)
            'Buckling factors taken from the same model with the two parts connected in the usual way without the extra node or the constraint equations.
            CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), 0.00002467401, 0.0001)
            CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(2), 0.0002220684, 0.00001)
            CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(3), 0.0006168994, 0.00001)
            CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(4), 0.001209392, 0.0001)
        End If

        'Using beams as trusses by coupling their displacement dofs. Also couples all dofs for a full beam joint.
        If SolveFile("B21 Constraint equations.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Compare buckling factor to the equivalent model using trusses in buckling 3D
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), 0.00180162163738604, 0.00003)
            'No displacement in the wrong directions.
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 4, 1), 0, 0.00000000001)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 5, 1), 0, 0.00000000001)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 6, 1), 0, 0.00000000001)
            'Displacement in X the same on coupled nodes
            CompareRatio(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 4, 1), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 5, 1), 0.00000000001)
            'Beam-beam joint should be rigid - displacement at the middle is half the end displacement. Same rotation angles
            CompareRatio(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 7, 1), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 5, 1) / 2, 0.00000000001)
            CompareRatio(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 7, 1), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 5, 1), 0.00000000001)
            CompareRatio(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 3, 1), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 5, 1), 0.00000000001)
            'Rotation at the pin joint matches displacement. theta = tan(theta) = u_x / length
            CompareRatio(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 5, 1), NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 5, 1) / 0.25, 0.00000000001)
        End If

        If SolveFile("B30 Constraint equations EulerSolid.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'Compare solution to the equivalent model without constraint equations
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 95, 1) / NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 62, 1), 3.616195 / 0.2883044)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 36, 1) / NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementZ, 46, 1), 2.46628 / 1.768739)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 95, 2) / NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 62, 2), -3.628052 / -0.2991126)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 36, 2) / NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementX, 46, 2), -2.475088 / -1.775968)
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), 2555.675)
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(2), 10123.3)
        End If


        'Also beam orientation vector and not aligned with axes.
        If SolveFile("B30 beam pinned-sliding and fixed-free.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'F = n π^2 E I / L^2
            Dim nsoft As Double = 1 'Pinned-sliding
            Dim nstiff As Double = 0.25 'Fixed-free
            Dim E As Double = 200000000000.0
            Dim L As Double = 3
            Dim Isoft As Double = 0.00000000026
            Dim Istiff As Double = 0.0000000065
            'Pinned-sliding mode
            CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), nsoft * Math.PI ^ 2 * E * Isoft / L ^ 2, 30)
            'Zero displacement at the nodes
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementMag, 1, 1), 0, 0.0000000000001)
            CompareDifference(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementMag, 2, 1), 0, 0.0000000000001)
            'Symmetric rotation about Z
            CompareRatio(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 1, 1),
                            -NodeValue(FieldVariableType.RotationZ, 2, 1), 0.00000000001)

            'Fixed-free mode
            CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(2), nstiff * Math.PI ^ 2 * E * Istiff / L ^ 2, 5)
        End If

        'This failed when Mystan had default DARPACK=2, requesting 2 additional modes which made it too many.
        If SolveFile("B30 beam pinned-sliding and fixed-free refined.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'F = n π^2 E I / L^2
            Dim nsoft As Double = 1 'Pinned-sliding
            Dim nstiff As Double = 0.25 'Fixed-free
            Dim E As Double = 200000000000.0
            Dim L As Double = 3
            Dim Isoft As Double = 0.00000000026
            Dim Istiff As Double = 0.0000000065
            'Pinned-sliding mode
            CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), nsoft * Math.PI ^ 2 * E * Isoft / L ^ 2, 0.001)
            'Fixed-free mode
            CompareRatio(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(3), nstiff * Math.PI ^ 2 * E * Istiff / L ^ 2, 0.0001)
        End If

        If SolveFile("B30 pinned support hex20.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            'F = n π^2 E I / L^2
            Dim n As Double = 1 'Pinned-pinned
            Dim E As Double = 210000000000
            Dim L As Double = 4
            Dim I As Double = 0.125 ^ 4 / 12
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(1), n * Math.PI ^ 2 * E * I / L ^ 2, 0.5)
            CompareRatioSingle(model.Modes.BucklingFactor(2), n * Math.PI ^ 2 * E * I / L ^ 2, 0.5)
        End If

        'Regression test for Mystran 17.0.0 bug
        'https://github.com/MystranSolver/MYSTRANSolver/issues/150
        If SolveFile("NAS S30 gravity node order.liml", testFilesFolder, model, solver) Then
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 1), -0.00000005)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 2), -0.00000005)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 5), -0.00000005)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 6), -0.00000005)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 9), -0.0000000125)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 10), -0.0000000125)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 13), -0.0000000125)
            CompareRatioSingle(NodeValue(FieldVariableType.DisplacementY, 14), -0.0000000125)
        End If


        Messages.Append("Number of tests")
        CompareDifference(TestCount - initialTestCount, 163)
        functionCount += 1

    End Sub '"
